Abstract: We introduce low-rank optimal control (LROC), a method for designing control pulses in open quantum systems whose full density-matrix simulation is prohibitively expensive. The method exploits a feature of quantum computing itself: because protocols are designed to preserve purity, the density matrix is dominated by a few pure states and admits an accurate low-rank factorization. LROC propagates only this factorized form and, by deriving the corresponding adjoint equation, obtains the gradient of any differentiable objective at the same reduced cost as the simulation, leading to a quadratic improvement in time and memory compared to the full master equation. We illustrate the breadth of the method on four superconducting-circuit tasks: preparation of a five-qubit GHZ state, a CNOT gate, qubit readout, and an error correction primitive, modeled with realistic multilevel transmons, decay, and strong drives, in each case reaching fidelities consistent with the intrinsic dissipation limits. LROC thereby extends pulse-level optimization to system sizes beyond the reach of existing gradient-based methods.

Abstract: Magic and entanglement are independent quantum resources, yet their exact relation in many-body dynamics has remained elusive. We uncover two structural principles. First, at any stabilizer state, the curvature of the second stabilizer Rényi entropy under an arbitrary Hermitian generator equals the quantum Fisher information up to a fixed normalization, creating a bidirectional bridge between computational and metrological resources. Second, for commuting Ising evolution on any forest graph, a Clifford pruning circuit yields the full stabilizer-Rényi family at arbitrary size and in any spatial embedding, thereby furnishing a graph-theoretic construction of families with finite magic density and vanishing entanglement density in the thermodynamic limit. We solve two paradigmatic one-dimensional realizations central to quantum simulation -- an Ising quench and a kicked Floquet chain -- exactly for arbitrary system size and directly in the thermodynamic limit, revealing finite magic density with vanishing entanglement density, distinct magic and entanglement revival periods, and Clifford points with zero magic but finite bipartite entanglement. The same tangent geometry fixes initial growth, perturbative revival lifting, and stability of thermodynamic magic minima. Large-scale Pauli-basis matrix-product-state calculations verify all predictions, and the tangent bridge yields a concrete protocol for detecting magic through established quantum-Fisher-information measurements.

</details>

Abstract: Understanding how complex systems transition between order and chaos is a central challenge of nonequilibrium physics. While weak perturbations of classical integrable systems give rise to a mixed phase space of coexisting regular and chaotic trajectories, analogous behavior in interacting quantum many-body systems has remained elusive. Here we develop and experimentally implement a hybrid quantum-classical feedback protocol that autonomously discovers and stabilizes long-lived regular trajectories in a superconducting quantum processor. Each iteration combines short-time quantum evolution with classical optimization that projects the dynamics back onto a low-entanglement variational manifold, effectively distilling coherence from chaotic evolution. The stabilized trajectories reveal a quantum many-body mixed phase space emerging from nonlinear variational dynamics, without a direct analogue in classical or few-body quantum systems. Our results establish a versatile framework for algorithmic discovery and control of coherent dynamics previously inaccessible to experiment.

Despite the many permutations of circuit geometry that have been explored, Josephson connectivities have so far been planar, making them effectively low-dimensional. The observed excitation spectrum shows excellent agreement with predictions from a neural network trained to generate variational quantum states, demonstrating that we have predictive power over our high-dimensional quantum system. Fine-structure splittings near the CGS point are compatible with weak tunneling or symmetry breaking due to experimental imperfections. We additionally use the superconducting cavity to externally induce symmetry breaking, observing a restoration of symmetry at the CGS point where ground states differ only by a $\mathbb{Z}_3$ phase. This work initiates a general program exploring lattice gauge theories using the toolbox of circuit quantum electrodynamics. More broadly, introducing non-planar Josephson connectivities opens a vast space for experimental and theoretical exploration of structures in almost any imaginable dimensionality and geometry.

Result: 在确定性实值态基准测试中，精确成本、精确梯度和已知全局最优下，Hopf优化器达到数值精度中位数差距。在VQE中平均最终差距更小，计量学启发的迹线在数值精度处更集中。

Conclusion: Hopf ansatz将通用态制备转化为任意纯态优化的可导航框架，梯度编译复杂度仅对数增长，测量预算作为独立统计代价。

Abstract: Optimizing arbitrary quantum state vectors is like navigating the unit sphere in Hilbert space: beyond target reachability, optimization asks for coordinates, local distance information, and measurable directions. We introduce the Hopf ansatz, a binary-tree circuit for arbitrary normalized real and complex state vectors. Internal angles steer probability between subtrees, leaf phases carry the complex degrees of freedom, and the same tree gives state preparation and an explicit inverse map from amplitudes to physical angles. Together these structures act as a compass for the search: the inverse map gives coordinates, the diagonal induced metric gives local distance information, and nonzero coordinate tangents become preparable normalized states.
  For Hamiltonian objectives, and for objectives with the same local transition-moment form, each gradient component is a known scale factor times a transition moment between the current state and a tangent state. A branch-state construction expresses these moments through expectation-value measurements, while the tree organizes the compiled gradient settings by magnitude layer and leaf-indexed phase family. Thus the number of distinct compiled gradient-access circuit families grows only logarithmically with Hilbert-space dimension, while the measurement budget required for a chosen precision remains a separate statistical cost. In deterministic real-state benchmarks with exact costs, exact gradients, and known global optima, metric-aware Hopf optimizers reach numerical-precision median gaps, with the clearest baseline gains in smaller VQE mean final gaps and stronger concentration of metrology-inspired traces at numerical precision. The Hopf ansatz turns universal state preparation into a navigable framework for arbitrary pure-state optimization.

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>

Abstract: In this work, we develop a coherent control technique for cavity QED based on amplitude amplification. We consider two physical platforms. In the first setting, we study a three-level quantum emitter coupled to a single mode of an optical cavity and introduce a protocol for producing traveling single photons based on oblivious amplitude amplification. As the key ingredient of our protocol, we propose an extension of oblivious amplitude amplification which uses reflection unitaries solely on the signal qubit, along with $U$ and $U^\dagger$, where the unitary $U$ prepares the initial state. Our approach improves the scaling of the single-photon-generation protocol length from $N\sim 1/p$ to $N\sim 1/\sqrt{p}$, with $p$ denoting the success probability of obtaining a short single photon from a single application of the weak control pulse. Furthermore, our protocol also reduces the error from intrinsic cavity loss compared to protocols using a single strong control pulse in various experimentally relevant regimes, suggesting the application of our methods for error reduction. In the second setting, we consider a superconducting qubit coupled to a single bosonic mode of a microwave cavity in a circuit QED architecture in the dispersive regime for preparing Fock states. Using fixed-point amplitude amplification, we obtain a protocol for preparing Fock states whose length scales as $O(n^{1/4})$, where $n$ is the number of photons. Additionally, as an application of our methods for state preparation, we describe a protocol for preparing NOON states.

</details>

Result: 尽管这三种噪声模型数学上等价，但由于硬件约束，它们在实际量子硬件上的实验结果存在显著差异。

Conclusion: 在量子网络上进行实验设计时必须谨慎选择噪声实现方法，因为硬件约束会导致数学等价的模型产生不同的实际性能结果。

Abstract: We investigate how quantum computers can be used to emulate quantum networks and study their performance under practical impairments. In particular, we evaluate how degraded entanglement and communication latency affect teleportation-based distributed multipartite-entanglement-state construction. We model imperfect Bell-pair sources using depolarizing noise channels and classical communication delays using thermal relaxation. We implement the depolarization using Stinespring dilation, randomly applied Pauli errors, and quasi-probability decompositions, evaluating the latter two on IQM quantum hardware and all three in simulation. We then study the performance of the entanglement distribution under noise generated by the aforementioned models. Although these noise models are mathematically equivalent, we find that hardware constraints result in profound differences in the corresponding results, highlighting the importance of careful experiment design.

</details>

Method: 使用扩张因果卷积神经网络(DCCNN)；训练数据为在Fermilab NEXUS站点测量的量子比特模板生成的合成Ramsey层析扫描；通过hls4ml工具将网络转换为FPGA固件，采用ap_fixed⟨16,6⟩量化；部署在Zynq UltraScale+ RFSoC ZCU216上。

Abstract: Ionizing radiation from cosmic rays and gammas can induce discontinuous jumps in the environmental charge of superconducting qubits (charge jumps), causing correlated errors that challenge fault-tolerant quantum computing while simultaneously providing a detection signature for quantum sensing applications. Current detection methods operate offline, introducing latency incompatible with in-the-loop qubit control. In this paper, an online detector of charge jumps for superconducting qubits, based on a dilated causal convolutional neural network (DCCNN) designed for in-the-loop deployment on the Quantum Instrumentation Control Kit (QICK) platform, is presented. The network is trained on synthetic Ramsey tomography scans generated from qubit templates measured at the Northwestern Experimental Underground Site (NEXUS) at Fermilab, and translated to FPGA firmware via hls4ml with ap_fixed$\langle 16,6 \rangle$ quantization, reaching a per-inference latency of $6.19 μ$s on the Zynq UltraScale+ RFSoC ZCU216. At this operating point the DCCNN matches the detection efficiency of the established offline $χ^2$ algorithm ($0.843 \pm 0.022$ vs. $0.866 \pm 0.020$ on $|Δq| \in [0.1, 0.5] e$ at matched false-positive rate), while requiring no per-qubit hyperparameter tuning. This shifts charge-jump detection from a post-hoc diagnostic to a control-loop primitive, enabling adaptive protocols that respond to radiation-induced events in situ, with applications to quantum-computing error mitigation and to the use of superconducting qubits as particle detectors.

### [93] [Disorder-enhanced compressibility of Floquet random quantum circuits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14296)

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前量子硬件受噪声和退相干限制，能够高保真实现的幺正电路深度有限。因此需要探索哪些动力学 regime 下时间演化算符更容易被浅层电路压缩，从而在近期含噪设备上实现更高效的量子模拟。

Method: 使用张量网络模拟一维 Floquet 随机电路，通过调节相互作用与无序强度的竞争来控制动力学 regime；计算 Floquet 幺正算符的算符纠缠熵以及无序时间关联函数（OTOC）的前沿传播来表征算符增长；优化浅层砖墙电路近似 Floquet 演化，比较不同无序强度下的压缩效果。

Result: 弱无序下算符快速 scrambling；强无序下 OTOC 前沿传播缓慢，算符纠缠熵呈对数或近似对数增长；在固定对数保真度密度下，强无序电路可压缩至远小于弱无序电路的深度。

Conclusion: 局域化或慢 scrambling 动力学为在含噪量子设备上进行压缩量子模拟提供了有利的 regime。

Abstract: Current quantum hardware is limited by noise and decoherence, which restrict the depth of unitary circuits that can be implemented with high fidelity. We investigate how the compressibility of time-evolution operators depends on the dynamical regime of the underlying many-body system. As a testbed, we study a one-dimensional Floquet random circuit with a tunable competition between interactions and on-site disorder. Using tensor-network simulations, we characterize operator growth through the operator-entanglement entropy of the Floquet unitary as well as of out-of-time-ordered correlators (OTOCs). We find rapid operator scrambling at weak disorder, while strong disorder leads to slow OTOC-front propagation and logarithmic or near-logarithmic operator-entanglement growth over the accessible time window. We then optimize shallow brickwall circuits to approximate the Floquet evolution and show that strong-disorder circuits can be compressed to substantially smaller depths than weak-disorder circuits at fixed logarithmic fidelity density. These results suggest that localized or slowly scrambling dynamics provide a favorable regime for compressed quantum simulation on noisy devices.

</details>


### [94] [An end-to-end quantum algorithm for weakly nonlinear plasma physics with superquadratic speedup](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14308)

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种端到端的量子算法，用于弱非线性动力学等离子体模型（3D电子-离子等离子体，含绝热电子、动力学离子、德拜屏蔽和Krook弛豫）。通过Carleman线性嵌入、层次化块编码协议和非线性信息提取子程序，实现了对时空平均动能的高效量子估计，在理论上证明了指数级内存节省和超二次时间加速，并建立了受控非线性等离子体的量子模拟基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非线性动力学等离子体模拟维度高、计算密集，经典计算面临巨大挑战；而量子算法存在三个瓶颈：将非线性动力学嵌入线性计算、加载稠密场-相互作用数据、高效提取信息。为此，作者希望设计一个端到端的量子算法来克服这些障碍。

Method: 1）利用等离子体自由能识别Lyapunov变换，使Carleman线性嵌入在认证的弱非线性区域内按截断阶数指数收敛；2）开发层次化块编码协议，利用场的空间衰减避免稀疏访问编码的多项式开销；3）引入信息提取子程序，利用完整Carleman历史状态中的非线性分量改进线性可观测量的估计。

Result: 量子算法估计时空平均动能所需门复杂度为$\widetilde{O}(N_F N_H^{1/2} \operatorname{polylog}(T/ε) 1/ε)$，量子比特数为$\widetilde{O}(\log(N_F N_H^{1/2}T) \log(1/ε))$。相比Fourier-Hermite谱求解器，实现了指数级内存节省和超二次时间加速。

Conclusion: 该工作建立了受控非线性等离子体的量子模拟基准，为量子算法在非线性等离子体物理中的应用提供了理论保证。

Abstract: Nonlinear kinetic plasma simulation is high-dimensional and classically demanding, while quantum algorithms face different bottlenecks: embedding nonlinear dynamics into a linear computation, loading dense field-interaction data, and efficiently extracting information. We present an end-to-end quantum algorithm, with rigorous convergence guarantees, for a weakly nonlinear kinetic plasma model. The system describes a 3D electron-ion plasma with adiabatic electrons, kinetic ions, Debye screening, and Krook relaxation. After Fourier-Hermite truncation, the dynamics reduces to a high-dimensional quadratic ordinary differential equation. To tackle quantum bottlenecks we combine three key ingredients. First, we use a plasma free energy to identify a Lyapunov transform under which a Carleman linear embedding converges exponentially in the truncation order within a certified weakly nonlinear regime. Second, we develop a hierarchical block-encoding protocol for dense matrices, exploiting the spatial decay of the field to avoid polynomial overhead from sparse access encodings. Third, we introduce a subroutine for information extraction that exploits nonlinear components encoded in the full Carleman history state to improve the estimation of linear observables. We construct a quantum algorithm to estimate the spacetime-averaged kinetic energy using $\widetilde{O}\!\left( N_F N_H^{1/2} \operatorname{polylog}\!\left(\frac{T}ε\right)\frac{1}ε\right)$ gates and $\widetilde{O}\!\left(\log\!\left(N_F N_H^{1/2}T\right)\log\!\left(\frac{1}ε\right)\right)$ qubits, where $N_F$ and $N_H$ are the Fourier and Hermite cutoffs. Relative to a Fourier-Hermite spectral solver, this yields exponential memory savings and superquadratic improvements in time. Together, these results establish a controlled nonlinear plasma benchmark for quantum simulation.

</details>


### [95] [General theory of monitored Quantum Reservoir Computing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14311)
*Oriol Morguí-Sancho,Gian Luca Giorgi,Gonzalo Manzano,Roberta Zambrini*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了基于间接量子测量的监测量子储备池计算（QRC）通用理论，统一了投影、弱、部分和耗散监测协议，建立了测量反作用作为可控资源的理论框架，推导了监测动力学满足回波状态、 fading memory 和输入可分性的条件，揭示了不同监测方案是计算能力的定性不同路径。

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有QRC研究将测量视为干扰源或计算资源，但不同监测方案被独立处理，缺乏统一的物理原理框架。

Method: 基于间接量子测量构建监测QRC的通用理论，统一多种监测协议，推导严格收缩性等判据，通过时间复用塑造信息提取与测量扰动之间的权衡。

Result: 建立了监测动力学满足回波状态、 fading memory 和输入可分性的一般判据；证明了不同监测协议不是可互换的参数化方案，而是定性不同的计算路径；测量反作用可作为可控资源，使原本不适合的未监测演化也能实现QRC。

Conclusion: 为在线监测量子储备池计算提供了统一理论基础，将量子测量工程确立为跨量子平台设计储备池架构的系统方法。

Abstract: Quantum reservoir computing (QRC) provides a powerful framework for processing temporal data using quantum dynamics, but incorporating measurements into the reservoir remains a fundamental challenge and distinctive feature with respect to classical settings. The induced back-action can vary from a source of disturbance to a computational resource, as measurement deeply modifies the dynamics underlying temporal processing. Existing approaches have treated specific monitoring schemes independently, missing the common physical principles governing online quantum reservoirs. Here we develop a general theory of monitored quantum reservoir computing based on indirect quantum measurements, which unifies projective, weak, partial, and dissipative monitoring protocols within a single operational framework. Measurement back-action can serve as a controllable resource, providing the effective dissipation and non-unital dynamics required for successful QRC, even when the underlying unmonitored evolution is unsuitable. We derive general criteria under which monitored dynamics satisfy the echo-state property, fading memory, and input separability, including a necessary and sufficient condition for emergent strict contractivity. By comparing different monitoring schemes under a common reference dynamics, we show that these protocols are not interchangeable parameterizations to be optimized for peak performance, but rather constitute qualitatively distinct routes to computational capability, each enabled by the interplay between information extraction and measurement-induced disturbance -- a trade-off that can be further shaped through time multiplexing. Our results provide a unified theoretical foundation for online monitored quantum reservoir computing and establish quantum measurement engineering as a systematic approach for designing reservoir architectures across different quantum platforms.

</details>


### [96] [Towards a monolithic platform for coupling superconducting circuits to low-loss microwave phonons in AlScN on 4H-SiC](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14319)
*Yuanchen Deng,William W. Roberts,Sueli Skinner-Ramos,Dalton Anderson,Katherine Hewey,Xingyu Du,Michael Miller,Brandon Smith,Hwijong Lee,Pingping Chen,Charles Thomas Harris,Roy H. Olsson,Lisa Hackett,Rupert Lewis,Matt Eichenfield*

TL;DR: 本文报道了一种单片集成的量子声学平台，在碳化硅（SiC）基底上结合铝超导电路和压电氮化铝钪（AlScN），实现了低声子损耗、强机电耦合与超导电路兼容性，无需悬浮结构。Al-on-SiC谐振器相干寿命达2.9 μs，AlScN-on-SiC声子寿命估计为7.6 μs，机电耦合系数约4.3%，为超导-声子混合量子系统提供了有前景的单片平台。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 混合超导-声子量子处理在腔QED、基于测量的量子计算等应用中前景广阔，但现有方案常需悬浮结构，影响可扩展性和超导电路性能。因此，需要一种无需悬浮、兼具低声子损耗、强机电耦合和超导电路兼容性的单片集成平台。

Method: 作者在SiC基底上选择性去除AlScN薄膜，在裸露SiC区域直接制备铝超导微波谐振器，保留相邻AlScN-on-SiC区域用于声子换能，实现单片集成架构。通过Al-on-SiC谐振器测量相干寿命，通过低温表面声波延迟线测量AlScN-on-SiC的声子传播损耗。

Conclusion: Al-on-SiC/AlScN-on-SiC是一种有前景的单片集成平台，可用于超导微波电路与压电声子组件的集成，支撑量子声学网络和混合量子系统的发展。

Abstract: Hybrid superconducting-phonon quantum processing is promising for cavity QED, measurement-based quantum computing, and other quantum applications. Relative to microwave photons at the same frequency, phonons can provide ultra-compact footprints, extremely low losses, and greater connectivity. Phonons can also couple strongly to superconducting circuits through the piezoelectric effect. However, this promise rests on scalable platforms that achieve these benefits without degrading superconducting circuit performance. This motivates a monolithic platform combining low phononic loss, strong electromechanical coupling, and superconducting-circuit compatibility without requiring suspended phononics. Here, we characterize a monolithic quantum acoustic platform combining aluminum superconducting circuits on exposed silicon carbide (SiC) with piezoelectric aluminum scandium nitride (AlScN) on SiC for integrated phononics. This architecture is enabled by selective removal of AlScN from selected chip regions, allowing aluminum superconducting microwave resonators to be fabricated directly on the SiC while preserving adjacent AlScN-on-SiC regions for phonon transduction. The resulting Al-on-SiC resonators exhibit a coherent lifetime of 2.9 μs, demonstrating compatibility with aluminum superconducting quantum devices. In parallel, cryogenic surface acoustic delay-line measurements on the retained AlScN-on-SiC regions show low phononic propagation loss at 4.05 GHz, corresponding to an estimated phonon lifetime of 7.6 μs. Together with a previously demonstrated electromechanical coupling coefficient of about 4.3% and a theoretical upper bound of 8%, these results establish Al-on-SiC/AlScN-on-SiC as a promising monolithic platform for integrating superconducting microwave circuits with piezoelectric phononic components for quantum acoustic networking and hybrid quantum systems.

</details>


### [97] [Collapse and Inversion of the Josephson Potential in a Strongly Driven Superconducting Circuit](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14344)
*Sercan Deve,Marius Villiers,Marijn A. Morssink,Robin C. Dekker,Gary A. Steele*

TL;DR: 本文通过工程化设计一种无寄生模式的transmon-谐振器系统，在高驱动功率下发现了约瑟夫森势垒的坍塌与反转现象，并证实了与倒立摆类似的动力学稳定机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 超导电路中增加微波驱动功率会引发非受控跃迁，现有通过电路对称性和参数选择来缓解的方法受限于寄生电路模式。本文旨在消除这些有害跃迁，探索高驱动功率下的新物理现象。

Method: 工程化设计一种transmon-谐振器系统，消除所有有害的非受控跃迁；通过光谱学和读实验测量驱动势垒的变化。

Result: 在高驱动功率下观测到约瑟夫森势垒的坍塌（势垒趋零）与反转（势垒反向），并证实transmon在势垒反转后在其不稳定平衡点处实现动力学稳定化，类似于倒立摆。

Conclusion: 该结果揭示了强驱动超导电路的新限制（超越驱动诱导跃迁），同时约瑟夫森势垒的动力学重整化为超导电路控制和噪声鲁棒量子态的自稳定提供了新途径。

Abstract: Superconducting circuits embedding Josephson junctions leverage microwave drives for control and measurement of quantum states. Although increasing the drive power is desirable for improving the efficiency of these operations, it eventually triggers unwanted transitions to uncontrolled states. While careful choice of circuit symmetries and parameters can mitigate these effects, the presence of spurious circuit modes spoils the resilience to high power. In this work, we engineer a transmon-resonator system free of any detrimental unwanted transitions. This resilience enables us to access drive powers at which we uncover a remarkable physical phenomenon: the collapse and inversion of the Josephson potential. Through spectroscopy and readout experiments, we confirm that the driven potential goes to zero and inverts as the power increases. The inversion corresponds to the dynamical stabilization of the transmon at its unstable equilibrium point, directly analogous to an inverted pendulum. This result reveals a new limitation of strongly driven superconducting circuits beyond drive-induced transitions. In addition, the dynamical renormalization of the Josephson potential opens up novel avenues for the control of superconducting circuits, and the autonomous stabilization of noise-resilient quantum states.

</details>


### [98] [Unified monogamy and polygamy relations for multipartite systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14370)
*Yue Cao,Naihuan Jing,Kailash Misra,Yiling Wang*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文针对满足γ次幂单配性不等式的纠缠度量E和满足δ次幂多配性不等式的辅助纠缠度量E_a，引入了一个由参数m≥1调控的统一框架，推导出两族层次化的精炼不等式：E的α次幂紧化单配性关系（α≥mγ）和E_a的β次幂紧化多配性关系（(m-1)δ<β≤mδ）。随着m增大，界限逐步收紧，在m=1时恢复已知结果。最优单配性界限表现为α的分段函数，当α跨越整阈值时激活修正项。通过concurrence及其辅助量进行了分析和数值验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有纠缠单配性与多配性不等式在不同幂次下缺乏统一的处理框架，且已有界限不够紧致，无法精确刻画多体纠缠分布特征。本文旨在构建一个可调参数的层次化框架，统一并推广已有结果，为量子通信和量子网络提供更灵活、更紧化的工具。

Method: 引入一个可调参数m≥1，基于已知的γ次幂单配性不等式和δ次幂多配性不等式，推导出α≥mγ条件下的单配性关系和(m-1)δ<β≤mδ条件下的多配性关系。通过分析α跨越整数阈值时修正项的激活机制，得到分段形式的最优单配性界限。使用concurrence和concurrence of assistance进行解析比较和数值评估。

Result: 推导了两族层次化的精炼不等式，界限随m增大而逐步收紧；最优单配性界限呈现为α的分段函数；数值实验表明本文结果比现有已知关系更紧、更普适。

Conclusion: 该参数化层次框架统一并推广了已有的纠缠单配性和多配性关系，提供了更紧且可调的工具，对量子通信、量子网络及多方量子信息处理具有重要的应用价值。

Abstract: For a bipartite entanglement measure $\mathcal{E}$ that satisfies the $γ$th-power monogamy inequality (Eq.~\eqref{e:chap1-ineq1}), and for its assisted counterpart $\mathcal{E}_a$ that obeys the $δ$th-power polygamy inequality (Eq.~\eqref{e:chap1-ineq2}), we introduce a unified, tunable framework indexed by a parameter $m\geq1$. Within this framework, we derive two hierarchical families of refined inequalities: 
  a tightened $α$-power monogamy relation for $\mathcal{E}$, valid for all $α\geq mγ$; 
  a tightened $β$-power polygamy relation for $\mathcal{E}_a$, applicable for $(m-1)δ< β\leq mδ$. 
  As $m$ increases, the bounds become progressively tighter, recovering known results at $m=1$. Notably, the optimal monogamy bound emerges as a piecewise function of $α$, with additional correction terms activated as $α$ crosses successive integer thresholds, thereby offering a sharper characterization of entanglement distribution.
  We demonstrate that our results generalize and strengthen existing monogamy and polygamy relations through analytical comparisons and numerical evaluations using concurrence and concurrence of assistance. This hierarchical, parameterized approach offers enhanced and flexible tools for applications in quantum communication, quantum networks, and multipartite quantum information processing.

</details>


### [99] [State-independent uncertainty relations on multipartite spin $1/2$-systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14376)
*Yiling Wang,Kailash Misra,Naihuan Jing*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文针对多体自旋-1/2系统，基于表示论框架（Clebsch-Gordan分解与总自旋方差极值分析）推导了精确的方差基态无关不确定关系（SIURs），首次建立了五体以下系统的统一代数框架，揭示了奇偶粒子数系统的结构二分性：奇数n系统具有严格正通用下界（如n=3时Δ²≥4/11），偶数n系统平凡扇区方差可为零但约化空间保持正下界（如n=4时Δ²≥1/8）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 精确的方差基SIURs此前仅局限于单体和二体系统，多体（三体及以上）情形在分析上一直未能解析解决，亟需建立超越二分体系的统一代数处理框架。

Method: 采用表示论框架，利用Clebsch-Gordan分解将集体自旋系统分解为不可约表示，通过总自旋方差极值分析（extremal analysis）推导精确的态无关下界。

Result: 推导出五体以下（n=2,3,4,5）的精确SIUR下界，发现奇偶系统结构二分性：奇数n有严格正通用下界（如n=3: 4/11, n=5: 12/29），偶数n在平凡扇区方差可为零但约化空间保持正下界（如n=2: 1/4, n=4: 1/8）。

Conclusion: 建立了首个多体方差基SIURs的统一代数框架，填补了该领域三体及以上系统的理论空白，为量子计量、密码学和纠缠检测提供了新的通用约束工具。

Abstract: Uncertainty relations quantify fundamental limits on simultaneous measurement of quantum observables. While conventional formulations are state-dependent, state-independent uncertainty relations (SIURs) impose universal bounds determined solely by the algebraic structure of the operators, with applications across metrology, quantum cryptography, and entanglement detection. Despite extensive study, exact analytical variance-based SIURs have so far been established primarily for one- and bi-partite systems, while entropic SIURs have been extended to multipartite and memory-assisted settings through information-theoretic constructions. In contrast, exact variance-based SIURs beyond the bipartite level have remained analytically unresolved.} Here we develop a representation-theoretic framework for multipartite SIURs in collective spin-$\tfrac{1}{2}$ systems. Using the Clebsch--Gordan decomposition and extremal analysis of total spin variance, we derive exact state-independent bounds up to quintipartite systems. A clear structural dichotomy emerges: odd $n$ systems exhibit strictly positive universal bounds (e.g., $Δ^2(\mathfrak{su}_2)\!\ge\!4/11$ for $n=3$), whereas even $n$ admit vanishing variance on trivial sectors but retain positive reduced-space bounds (e.g., $Δ^2(\mathfrak{su}_2)\!\ge\!1/8$ for $n=4$). These results establish the first unified, algebraic framework for multipartite variance-based SIURs in qubit ensembles.

</details>


### [100] [Circuit complexity lower bounds for quantum spin glasses](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14384)
*Omar Al-Ghattas,David Gamarnik*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文证明了量子p-自旋玻璃模型中，制备接近最优能量的基态需要深度Ω_p(log n)的量子电路（当平均相互作用度随n增长时），或在固定深度下需要足够大的度前缀（当平均度有界时），从而表明浅层电路无法产生闭合乘积态能隙所需的纠缠，这为随机量子自旋玻璃模型提供了No Low-Energy Trivial States（NLTS）类的阻障结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子信息理论中的一个核心问题是标准多体模型所产生态（states arising from standard many-body models）的电路复杂度。本文特别关注量子p-自旋玻璃模型，此前Anschuetz等人已证明其最优能量与乘积态能达到的最佳能量之间存在间隙，但浅层电路（甚至深度为一的电路）可能通过生成纠缠来缩小该间隙，这一开放性问题是本文研究的动机。

Method: 本文采用概率方法，将随机量子自旋玻璃哈密顿量的态制备下界重新表述为以浅层电路为索引的高斯过程的均匀控制（uniform control of Gaussian processes indexed by shallow circuits）。具体针对两种情形分别证明：1）平均相互作用度随n增长时，对所有足够大的固定p，任何制备能量接近最优态的n量子比特电路必须具有深度Ω_p(log n)；2）在有界平均度情形下，证明固定深度的阻障：对每个固定深度D，足够大的度前缀会阻止深度D电路制备近基态。这些结论对任意数量的辅助量子比特（ancilla qubits）电路均成立。

Result: 本文获得两个主要结果：1）当平均相互作用度随n增长时，对于所有足够大的固定p，制备归一化能量在最优值固定正常数范围内的n量子比特态，所需电路深度为Ω_p(log n)；2）在有界平均度区域，对每个固定深度D，足够大的度前缀会排除深度D电路制备近基态的可能性。两个结果均对任意数量辅助量子比特的电路一致成立。

Conclusion: 本文结果为随机量子自旋玻璃模型提供了类似Freedman和Hastings提出的"No Low-Energy Trivial States (NLTS)"问题的阻障，但与基于纠错码的哈密顿量（如Anshu等人的工作，其基态存在多项式规模制备电路）不同，本文针对的是随机量子自旋玻璃模型。这一设定为NLTS类问题开辟了一条概率路径，将随机量子哈密顿量的态制备下界转化为以浅层电路为索引的高斯过程的均匀控制问题。

Abstract: A central question in quantum information theory is the circuit complexity of states arising from standard many-body models. We study this question for quantum $p$-spin glasses, random Hamiltonians whose interactions act on $p$-tuples of qubits through Pauli strings. Anschuetz, Gamarnik, and Kiani (arXiv:2404.07231) showed that the optimum energy is separated from the best energy achievable by product states. This leaves open whether shallow circuits can close the gap, since even depth-one circuits can generate entanglement.
  We show that the entanglement needed to close the product-state gap cannot be generated at shallow depth. When the average interaction degree grows with $n$, we prove that, for all sufficiently large fixed $p$, any circuit preparing an $n$-qubit state whose normalized energy is within a fixed positive constant of the optimum must have depth $Ω_p(\log n)$. In the bounded-average-degree regime, we prove a fixed-depth obstruction: for every fixed $D$, a sufficiently large degree prefactor rules out depth-$D$ preparation of near-ground states. Both results hold uniformly over circuits with an arbitrary number of ancilla qubits.
  Our results give an obstruction in the spirit of the No Low-Energy Trivial States problem of Freedman and Hastings (arXiv:1301.1363), but for random quantum spin glasses rather than code-based Hamiltonians such as those of Anshu, Breuckmann, and Nirkhe (arXiv:2206.13228), whose ground states admit polynomial-size preparation circuits. This setting opens a probabilistic route to NLTS-like questions: we recast state-preparation lower bounds for random quantum Hamiltonians as uniform control of Gaussian processes indexed by shallow circuits.

</details>


### [101] [Efficient quantum algorithm for Heisenberg spin systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14401)
*Sergey Bravyi,David Gosset,Yinchen Liu,Benjamin Wong*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究Suzuki-Fisher (1971)提出的一类海森堡型量子自旋系统（包括任何二分图上的海森堡反铁磁体），证明了其基态和吉布斯态是半径为1的李-杨张量，并推导出激发态与基态能隙的下界为2μ（μ为Z方向磁场）。基于此，提出了一个高效的量子绝热算法来计算该族模型的基态能量。证明核心是建立了正半定算子能隙与其李-杨半径之间的新不等式关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 揭示李-杨张量（Lee-Yang tensor）与量子自旋系统基态能量的深层联系，并利用这种零自由性质来推导能隙下界，进而设计高效的量子绝热算法。

Method: 将基态和吉布斯态建模为与多重线性多项式关联的李-杨张量（半径1），利用多项式在单位多圆盘内的零自由性质；证明了一个新的不等式，将正半定算子的谱间隙与其李-杨半径联系起来，从而得到能隙下界。

Result: 对该族哈密顿量，第一激发态与基态能隙被证明至少为2μ（μ为Z方向磁场）；基于此能隙下界，获得了一个高效的量子绝热算法。

Conclusion: 李-杨半径方法可定量地刻画能隙，为量子自旋系统的谱分析提供了新工具，且本文建立的能隙-李-杨半径不等式可能在更广泛的领域有应用价值。

Abstract: We consider a broad family of Heisenberg-type quantum spin systems that were studied by Suzuki and Fisher in 1971. This family includes, for example, the Heisenberg antiferromagnet on any bipartite graph. The ground and Gibbs states of these models are Lee-Yang tensors with radius 1: they are associated with multilinear polynomials that possess an extraordinary zero-freeness property inside the unit polydisk in the complex plane. For each Hamiltonian in this family we show that the spectral gap between the first-excited and ground-state energies is lower bounded by $2μ$, where $μ$ is the magnetic field along the $Z$ direction. Using this result we obtain an efficient quantum adiabatic algorithm for the ground energy of any model in this family. The proof is based on a new inequality that relates the spectral gap of a positive semidefinite operator to its Lee-Yang radius -- a quantitative strengthening of prior work of the authors that may find applications elsewhere.

</details>


### [102] [Imaginarity as a Resource within Quantum Coherence: Geometric Decomposition and Operational Conversion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14435)
*Meng-Li Guo,Nannan Guan,Bo Li,Bin Hu,Shao-Ming Fei*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文建立了一个严格的几何框架，将虚数性（imaginarity）识别为量子相干性的基本资源。通过几何分解将相干性划分为虚数分量和残余分量，揭示了这些资源之间的普适层级关系。对于双粒子系统，该分解提供了非局域关联与虚数性限制局域相干性生成的确切界。文章还设计了将虚数性转化为可用相干性的操作协议，并刻画了对角哈密顿量下的动力学演化，证明总相干性守恒而虚数性与残余相干性呈现互补振荡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有量子相干性理论中，虚数性（量子态密度矩阵中虚部所携带的资源）尚未被严格识别和量化。本文旨在建立严密的框架，将虚数性确立为量子相干性中一种基础性的资源成分，并揭示其与总相干性、残余相干性之间的结构关系。

Method: 采用几何分解方法，将相干性分解为虚数分量和残余分量；利用该分解推导双粒子系统中非局域关联与虚数性对局域相干性生成的约束边界；设计显式的操作协议实现虚数性到可用相干性的转换；通过分析对角哈密顿量下的动力学演化，刻画虚数性与残余相干性的互补振荡关系。

Result: 建立了虚数性作为量子相干性基本资源的严格几何框架；揭示了总相干性、虚数性与残余相干性之间的普适层级关系；给出了双粒子系统中非局域关联与虚数性对局域相干性的定量界；证明了虚数性到相干性的直接可转换性；发现对角哈密顿量下总相干性守恒，而虚数性与残余相干性呈互补振荡。

Conclusion: 虚数性是量子相干性中不可忽视的基本资源成分，几何框架和理论界具有普适性，而转换协议和动力学分析为量子比特系统提供了原理性验证。该工作为分布式量子技术中的资源管理提供了具体的几何和操作层面的洞见。

Abstract: We establish a rigorous framework that identifies imaginarity as a fundamental resource inherent in quantum coherence. By means of a geometric decomposition, we partition coherence into distinct imaginarity and residual components, thereby revealing a universal hierarchical relationship among these resources. For bipartite systems, this decomposition provides explicit bounds on the extent to which nonlocal correlations and imaginarity limit local coherence generation. Furthermore, we devise an explicit operational protocol that converts imaginarity into usable coherence, demonstrating the direct interconvertibility of these resources under physically admissible operations. The dynamical evolution under diagonal Hamiltonians is fully characterized, showing that while total coherence is conserved, imaginarity and residual coherence exhibit complementary oscillations. Our results provide a rigorous geometric and operational characterization of imaginarity as a fundamental constituent of quantum coherence, offering concrete insights for resource management in distributed quantum technologies. The geometric framework and theoretical bounds established herein are fully general, while the explicit conversion protocol and dynamical analysis serve as a compelling proof-of-principle demonstration in qubit systems.

</details>


### [103] [Improving Dynamical Decoupling for Trapped-Ion QCCD Quantum Computers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14441)
*William M. Watkins,Leigh M. Norris,Ross Hutson,Maxwell Urmey,Peter Siegfried,Charles H. Baldwin*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了量子电荷耦合器件(QCCD)中调度误差对动态去耦(DD)的影响，发现调度引起的时间误差会增强DD对低频波动的敏感性，导致超过2Hz的DD脉冲频率通常适得其反。作者提出了一种实时DD协议，在空闲期间机会性地插入重聚焦脉冲，并在Quantinuum H2-1上通过Ramsey延迟实验进行了验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在QCCD架构中，量子比特传输和控制引入的随机脉冲延迟会影响DD的效率，因此需要分析调度误差对DD性能的影响并开发更优的存储错误抑制策略。

Method: 采用滤波器函数形式化方法分析调度诱导的时间误差对DD性能的影响，并提出一种实时DD协议，在空闲期间机会性地插入重聚焦脉冲。实验上在Quantinuum H2-1平台上通过Ramsey延迟实验进行了验证。

Result: 调度引起的时间误差增加了标准DD对低频波动的敏感性；在典型的离子阱存储错误场景下，DD脉冲频率超过2Hz通常是适得其反的。

Conclusion: 通过实时DD协议可以更有效地抑制QCCD架构中的存储错误，优于传统固定频率的DD方案。

Abstract: We examine the impact of scheduling errors on dynamical decoupling (DD) in trapped-ion quantum charge-coupled devices (QCCDs) and develop better strategies for reducing memory errors. In the QCCD architecture, qubit transport and control introduce stochastic pulse delays that impact the efficiency of DD. Using the filter-function formalism, we analyze the performance of DD in the presence of scheduling-induced timing errors, showing that they increase the sensitivity of standard DD to low-frequency fluctuations. For typical memory errors in trapped-ion platforms, we numerically demonstrate that increasing the DD pulse frequency beyond 2 Hz is generally counterproductive due to scheduling-induced timing errors. Furthermore, we introduce a real-time DD protocol that inserts refocusing pulses opportunistically during idle periods. We demonstrate our methods on Quantinuum H2-1 with Ramsey delay-type experiments.

</details>


### [104] [Quantum correlations of tripartite mixed states in the black hole quantum atmosphere](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14462)
*Anqi Zhang,Yanze Zheng,Xiaofen Huang,Tinggui Zhang*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了黑洞量子大气中三体混合态的量子态纹理、真多体纠缠和三体非定域性，发现这三者的极值均位于近地平线区域，并随局域霍金温度升高而外移，三体非定域性比真多体纠缠更脆弱。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究黑洞量子大气对三体混合态中不同量子信息方面（量子态纹理、真多体纠缠、三体非定域性）的影响，并建立统一表征。

Method: 将Hartle-Hawking局域温度引入Bogoliubov系数，表征局域霍金效应对物理可及和不可及约化态的影响。

Result: 三种量的极值均位于近地平线区域，随局域霍金温度升高而外移，与局域霍金温度峰值区域一致；三体非定域性比真多体纠缠更脆弱，受更强局域霍金效应抑制。

Conclusion: 这些结果为受黑洞量子大气影响的三体混合态提供了密度矩阵重构、纠缠重分配和非定域性的统一表征。

Abstract: We investigate quantum state texture, genuine multipartite entanglement, and tripartite nonlocality of a tripartite mixed state in the black hole quantum atmosphere. By introducing the Hartle-Hawking local temperature into the Bogoliubov coefficients, we characterize the influence of the local Hawking effect on both physically accessible and inaccessible reduced states. We find that the extrema of these three quantities all lie in the same near horizon region and shift outward with increasing local Hawking temperature, coinciding with the peak region of the local Hawking temperature and indicating that different aspects of tripartite quantum information are most sensitive to the local Hawking effect in the same atmospheric region. In contrast to genuine multipartite entanglement, tripartite nonlocality is more fragile and is suppressed under stronger local Hawking effects. These results provide a unified characterization of density matrix restructuring, entanglement redistribution, and nonlocality in tripartite mixed states affected by the black hole quantum atmosphere.

</details>


### [105] [Perturbation Analysis of Maximal Quantum Leakage](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14469)
*Zijia Zhao,Shuixin Xiao,Farhad Farokhi*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了最大量子泄漏(MQL)在量子编码扰动下的敏感性，建立了基于迹距离的连续性界限，并证明了该界限的可达性，进一步推导了基于保真度和相对熵的充分条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 最大量子泄漏(MQL)作为一种最坏情况下的信息泄露度量，虽然对给定的量子态系综具有精确刻画，但其对实现不完美性的鲁棒性尚未被系统研究。

Method: 通过分析量子编码扰动下MQL的敏感性，建立了基于理想与扰动量子态间迹距离的连续性界限，并通过示例证明该界限是可达到的；同时推导了基于保真度和相对熵的充分条件。

Result: 建立了MQL在迹距离下的连续性界限并证明其可达性；给出了基于保真度和相对熵的充分条件，数值实验表明这些条件可能是松的。

Conclusion: MQL对量子编码扰动具有可量化的鲁棒性，迹距离界是紧的，而保真度和相对熵条件相对保守。

Abstract: Maximal quantum leakage (MQL) is a worst-case information leakage measure that quantifies an adversary's inference advantage gained from accessing quantum encoding of classical data with arbitrary measurements. While MQL admits an exact characterization for a given ensemble of quantum states, its robustness to implementation imperfections has not been systematically studied. In this paper, we analyze the sensitivity of maximal quantum leakage under perturbations of the quantum encoding. We establish a continuity bound in terms of the trace distance between ideal and perturbed quantum states, and show, via an example, that this bound is attainable. We further derive fidelity-based and relative-entropy-based sufficient conditions for bounding the variation of maximal quantum leakage, and illustrate numerically that these conditions can be loose.

</details>


### [106] [A generalized variational quantum linear solver on photonic platform](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14477)
*Kang Gao,Zhao-An Wang,Ze-Guo Wang,Mao-Mao Huang,Hai Wei,Kai Wen*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该研究在光子计算平台上实验验证了广义变分量子线性求解器（VQLS），系统求解了复数域上的四维线性方程组（包括奇异病态问题）和有限域F2上的线性系统，展示了VQLS在实际量子计算中的应用潜力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VQLS主要针对复数域非奇异系统，而实际应用中常遇到奇异病态问题，且有限域上的线性求解在编码理论等领域有重要需求，因此需要扩展VQLS的适用范围。

Method: 1) 引入Tikhonov正则化启发的扰动项处理奇异病态系统；2) 重新设计代价函数融入模2运算并施加约束，将VQLS扩展到有限域F2。

Result: 在光子计算平台上成功实验验证了复数域（含非奇异和奇异病态系统）及有限域F2上的四维线性方程求解，证明了方法的有效性和普适性。

Conclusion: 该工作为VQLS的实际应用提供了坚实的实验基础和方法指导，展示其在量子计算中的实用潜力。

Abstract: Based on a photonic computing platform, we experimentally validate a generalized variational quantum linear solver (VQLS) by systematically solving four-dimensional linear equation systems across different fields. In the complex field, in addition to solving non-singular systems that admit a unique solution, we investigate ill-conditioned problems arising from singularity--an issue frequently encountered in practical applications. To tackle these challenges, we introduce perturbation terms, a treatment inspired by Tikhonov regularization, and develop an algorithm capable of handling a wide range of systems. Furthermore, we extend the VQLS to the finite field F2 by redesigning the cost function to incorporate modulo 2 and imposing several constraints on the solution vector. This modulo 2 VQLS is inherently free from singularity. It is adapt to stabilizer coding theory and may find applications in areas such as decoders and the design of quantum gate sequences. Therefore, our work demonstrates the practical potential of VQLS in quantum computing, providing a solid experimental foundation and methodological guidance for its real-world applications.

</details>


### [107] [Syndrome-as-Header: A Quantum Label-Switching Architecture via Uncorrectable Error Injection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14484)
*IlKwon Sohn,Ju-Bong Kim,Kwangil Bae,Wooyeong Song,Wonhyuk Lee*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出 Syndrome-as-Header (SAH) 架构，通过将路由标签嵌入量子负载的校验子结构中，利用不可纠正错误注入（UEI）构建校验子空间头码本，实现无需与外部经典头部对齐的量子标签交换，在时序不确定性下优于传统光突发交换和量子封装包交换方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有量子网络在点对点链路之外扩展时，依赖外部经典头部的光突发交换（OBS）和量子封装包交换（QW）存在关键缺陷：经典头部处理易受抖动影响，必须与飞行中的量子负载保持对齐，导致保护窗口或光纤延迟线预算失效。本文旨在消除这种对齐依赖，提升量子网络在时序不确定性下的转发可靠性。

Method: 提出 Syndrome-as-Header (SAH) 架构：1) 利用不可纠正错误注入（UEI）将流标签映射到参考校验子；2) 构建校验子空间头码本，其解码区域在可纠正信道诱导校验子偏差下仍可区分；3) 核心路由器提取校验子头部，抑制可纠正残差信道错误分量，在不测量或解码逻辑负载的情况下交换标签；4) 支持 FAST 转发和 VERIFICATION 验证两种模式。

Result: 在 NSFNet 时序基准测试中，结合常见每跳量子纠错（QEC），SAH 消除了外部头部/负载对齐条件，时序不确定性表现为内存驻留和交付延迟而非对齐预算下降。在基于 T₂ 的内存驻留惩罚下，优势取决于路由器内存相干时间；当相干时间足够长时，SAH 在高抖动条件下保持比 OBS+QEC 和 QW+QEC 更高的接受率和吞吐量。

Conclusion: SAH 通过将路由标签嵌入量子纠错码的校验子结构，从根本上解决了量子网络转发中对齐依赖问题，为高时序不确定性环境下的量子网络扩展提供了更鲁棒的标签交换方案。

Abstract: Scaling quantum networks beyond point-to-point links requires packet forwarding that can tolerate control-plane timing uncertainty. Existing optical-burst switching (OBS) and quantum-wrapper-based packet switching (QW) rely on external classical headers whose jitter-prone processing must remain aligned with in-flight quantum payloads, creating guard-window or fiber-delay-line budget failures. This paper proposes Syndrome-as-Header (SAH), a quantum label-switching architecture that embeds routing labels into the syndrome structure of an encoded payload. The proposed scheme uses Uncorrectable Error Injection (UEI) to map flow labels to reference syndromes and builds a syndrome-space header codebook whose decoding regions remain distinguishable under correctable channel-induced syndrome deviations. Core routers extract the syndrome header, suppress the correctable residual channel-error component, and swap labels without measuring or decoding the logical payload. SAH supports FAST forwarding and VERIFICATION mode, the latter providing end-to-end consistency checking while retaining swappable labels. In NSFNet timing benchmarks with common per-hop quantum error correction (QEC), SAH eliminates the external-header/payload alignment condition, so timing uncertainty appears as memory residence and delivered latency rather than alignment-budget drops. With a $T_2$-based memory-residence penalty, the advantage depends on router memory coherence; for sufficiently long coherence, SAH maintains higher acceptance and throughput than OBS+QEC and QW+QEC under high jitter.

</details>


### [108] [Geometric Mode Steering of the Quantum Mpemba Effect](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14501)
*Yingying Hong,Longxing Xu,Weiwei Zhang,Jie Ren,Jianhui Wang*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出一种预耗散几何导引协议，通过在弛豫开始前对量子态施加相干旋转和非选择性投影测量，抑制与最慢Liouvillian模式的重叠，从而在固定Lindblad生成器下实现量子Mpemba效应——初始状态离平衡更远但弛豫更快。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开放量子系统弛豫到稳态通常受最慢Liouvillian模式瓶颈限制。现有策略需选择特殊初态或工程化耗散器，作者旨在证明两者皆非必要，探索在固定耗散条件下加速弛豫的普适方案。

Method: 提出预耗散几何导引协议：在弛豫开始前，对任意纯态或混态施加相干旋转与非选择性投影测量（沿测地线路径导引布洛赫方向），通过重塑态的空间分布以抑制与最慢Liouvillian模式的重叠。框架统一处理实部和复部谱隙，导出两个可计算条件（减小的慢模重叠和更大的初始稳态距离）作为Mpemba效应判据。

Result: 在驱动量子比特和多量子比特系统中，利用囚禁离子和超导平台可用的操作验证了鲁棒的Mpemba加速效应。协议不依赖于特殊初态选择或耗散器工程，在固定Lindblad生成器下仍能实现更快的弛豫。

Conclusion: 该工作提供了一种无需修改耗散结构即可加速开放量子系统弛豫的通用几何方法，为量子热化和量子信息处理中的非平衡动力学控制开辟新途径。

Abstract: The slowest Liouvillian mode often bottlenecks the relaxation of an open quantum system to its steady state. Standard strategies circumvent this bottleneck by selecting special initial states or engineering the dissipator. Here we show that neither is necessary. We introduce a pre-dissipative geometric steering protocol that reshapes any given pure or mixed state before relaxation begins -- coherent rotations interleaved with nonselective projective measurements -- at fixed Lindblad generator. By steering the state's Bloch direction along geodesic paths, the protocol suppresses its overlap with the slowest Liouvillian modes. The prepared state then starts farther from equilibrium yet relaxes faster, realizing the quantum Mpemba effect, whenever two computable conditions hold: reduced slow-mode overlap and a larger initial distance to stationarity. Our framework treats real and complex spectral gaps uniformly, and we demonstrate robust Mpemba acceleration in driven qubit and multiqubit systems using operations available in trapped-ion and superconducting platforms.

</details>


### [109] [Structure-Aware Variational State Preparation for Quantum Basket Option Pricing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14518)
*Dongwoo Kim,Zhenyu Cui,Daniel K. Park,Chihoon Lee*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 针对量子幅度估计在篮子期权定价中受状态制备电路深度限制的问题，提出了一种基于张量列车秩信息的结构感知量子状态制备框架，利用浅层变分电路实现指数级深度到线性深度缩减，同时保持低误差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 篮子期权定价通常依赖蒙特卡洛估计，量子幅度估计(QAE)可提供二次加速，但实际收益受限于状态制备电路的深度，亟需设计浅层化的状态制备方案以发挥QAE的实际优势。

Method: 提出结构感知量子状态制备框架：(1) 利用张量列车(TT)秩信息设计浅层变分电路，在独立情况下去除硬件高效拟设中不必要的纠缠连接；(2) 在相关篮子设定中，局部制备资产边缘分布，训练紧凑潜变量块以匹配篮子累积分布函数(Basket-CDF)，直接对齐状态制备与篮子依赖的收益函数。

Result: 数值实验表明，所提出的电路将精确幅度加载的指数级状态制备深度缩放替换为线性缩放，同时保持低百分比的篮子定价误差；额外的基于采样的训练实验和端到端QAE集成研究支持与样本估计训练及标准QAE定价工作流的兼容性。

Conclusion: 该框架有效缓解了QAE在篮子期权定价中状态制备的深度瓶颈，实现了深度从指数到线性的缩减，为量子金融中实际可用的加速定价提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Basket option pricing often relies on Monte Carlo estimation, for which quantum amplitude estimation (QAE) provides a quadratic speed-up. However, the practical benefit of QAE can be limited by the depth of the state-preparation circuit. We propose a structure-aware quantum state-preparation framework for QAE-based basket option pricing. The framework uses tensor-train (TT) rank information to design shallow variational state-preparation circuits. In the independent regime, TT ranks remove unnecessary entangling links from a hardware-efficient ansatz. In correlated basket settings, we instead prepare asset-wise marginals locally and train a compact latent block to match the basket cumulative distribution function. The Basket-CDF objective targets the basket pushforward distribution rather than the full joint state, directly aligning state preparation with basket-dependent payoffs. Numerical experiments show that the proposed circuits replace the exponential state-preparation depth scaling of exact amplitude loading with linear scaling, while maintaining low-percent basket-pricing errors. Additional sampling-based training experiments and an end-to-end QAE integration study support compatibility with sample-estimated training and standard QAE-based pricing workflows.

</details>


### [110] [No Finite NPA Level Characterizes the Complete Quantum Set in the Simplest Bell Scenario](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14569)
*Anubhav Chaturvedi*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文证明了在双粒子两测量场景下，NPA层级中任何有限层都无法精确刻画量子行为集合，即使CHSH和单边倾斜CHSH最大值可用有限层精确描述，但完整量子集在最小场景中无法用有限层精确描述。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: NPA层级是量子行为的标准半定外部逼近方法，但长期以来，即使在双粒子两二值测量的最小场景中，任何有限层是否能够等于量子集一直是一个悬而未决的问题。

Method: 通过构造对称双倾斜CHSH泛函h_α，分析其量子最大值ω_Q与固定NPA层级最大值ω_L的渐近行为差异。利用边界重标度下的正算子期望收敛到Motzkin多项式这一性质，证明若固定层级误差有界，则Motzkin多项式加非负常数会成为平方和，这与已知结论矛盾。

Result: 证明了任何有限NPA层级都无法精确等于量子集；非量子行为在局部确定性行为处累积；所有基于固定有限词列表的标准NPA松弛都严格包含完整量子集。

Conclusion: 即使在最小场景（双粒子、每方两个二值测量）中，CHSH和单边倾斜CHSH的有限层精确性也无法推广到完整量子集的精确有限层描述，即没有固定有限层的NPA松弛可以精确刻画完整量子集。

Abstract: The Navascués--Pironio--Acín (NPA) hierarchy gives the standard semidefinite outer approximations to quantum behaviors. Whether \emph{any} finite level can already equal the quantum set has remained open even in the bipartite scenario with two binary measurements per party. We demonstrate that \emph{no finite level} is exact. For the symmetric doubly tilted CHSH functional $h_α=A_0B_0+A_0B_1+A_1B_0-A_1B_1+α(A_0+B_0)$, set $T=1-α$. Its quantum maximum satisfies $[ω_{\rm Q}(1-T)-(4-2T)]/T^3\to4/3$, whereas every fixed NPA level satisfies $[ω_L(1-T)-ω_{\rm Q}(1-T)]/T^3\to+\infty$. Under the corresponding boundary rescaling, an explicit expectation of the positive operator $ω_{\rm Q}(1-t^2)I-H_t$ converges to the Motzkin polynomial. A bounded fixed-level error would therefore make the Motzkin polynomial plus a nonnegative constant a sum of squares, which is impossible. Consequently, every standard NPA relaxation based on a fixed finite list of words in the measurement projectors strictly contains the complete quantum set, and its nonquantum behaviors accumulate at a local deterministic behavior. Thus, the finite-level exactness of CHSH and all one-sided tilted CHSH maxima does not extend to an exact finite-level description of the complete quantum set in the minimal scenario.

</details>


### [111] [Exact No Signaling in Time without Temporal Classicality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14583)
*Minsu Kim,Jeongho Bang,Han Seb Moon*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文证明，时间序列中的无信号条件（NSIT）即使精确满足，也不能保证过程是经典的。通过引入非选择性测量通道的公共不动点（CFP）机制，作者展示了一个qutrit环系统在满足精确配对NSIT的同时，仍然可以违反Leggett-Garg不等式，包括最大混合态也能产生量子违背，彻底推翻"NSIT等价于经典性"的传统观点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 澄清长期以来将NSIT（时间无信号条件）视为非侵入可测量性操作遗迹的误解，证明精确NSIT并不足以保证时间过程具有经典性。

Method: 引入非选择性测量通道的公共不动点（CFP）作为精确机制，在qutrit环上对局部简并Lüders测量进行完整的CFP流形解析求解，并通过隐变量重构、熵见证、协议景观扫描、噪声鲁棒性和有限shot模拟进行验证。

Result: CFP流形上的每一个态都满足精确的配对NSIT，但每个非平凡成员（包括最大混合态）都违反Leggett-Garg不等式，且违背由有限分支位移驱动而非残余信号传递。

Conclusion: 精确NSIT仅仅认证了边际信号在结果擦除后的消失，并不能证明经典历史的存在；量子非经典性可以在完全无信号的条件下持续存在。

Abstract: No signaling in time (NSIT) has often been treated as the clean operational remnant of noninvasive measurability. If an earlier measurement leaves every later marginal unchanged, the temporal process appears classical. We show that this inference is false. We introduce common fixed points (CFPs) of nonselective measurement channels as an exact mechanism that erases all marginal evidence of invasiveness while preserving disturbed outcome conditioned branches. For a qutrit ring subject to a local degenerate Luders measurement, we solve the full CFP manifold analytically. Every state on this manifold satisfies exact pairwise NSIT, yet every nontrivial member violates a Leggett Garg inequality, including the maximally mixed state. The violation is governed by finite branch displacement, not by residual signaling. Hidden variable reconstruction, entropic witnesses, protocol landscape scans, noise robustness, and finite shot simulations show that exact NSIT certifies only the disappearance of marginal signals after outcome erasure, not the existence of a classical temporal history.

</details>


### [112] [Adaptive Entanglement Management in Quantum Multi-Core Architectures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14592)
*Rajeswari Suance P S,Anubhab Dutta,Ruchika Gupta,John Jose*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文比较了三种量子纠缠管理范式（按需生成ODG、持续预生成CGP、自适应持续预生成ACGP），提出ACGP根据通信模式动态调整纠缠生成概率，在mesh架构多核量子处理器上通过仿真验证，ACGP显著降低量子隐形传态延迟，且纠缠纯化可有效恢复因存储导致的保真度下降。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多核量子计算架构中，核间通信依赖量子隐形传态，而纠缠生成效率是性能瓶颈。现有方案（ODG、CGP）无法兼顾延迟与资源效率，因此需要自适应纠缠管理策略来提升通信效率。

Method: 提出自适应连续预生成（ACGP）方法，根据观察到的核间通信模式动态调整纠缠生成概率；在扩展的SeQUeNCe仿真器上，基于mesh架构多核系统，使用真实基准电路对ODG、CGP和ACGP三种范式进行对比评估。

Result: ACGP相比ODG和CGP显著降低了平均量子隐形传态延迟；虽然预生成引入因存储时间导致的保真度下降，但纠缠纯化能有效恢复保真度，且对延迟影响很小。

Conclusion: 自适应纠缠管理能够显著提升可扩展量子多核系统的通信效率，为未来多核量子计算架构的设计提供了有效方案。

Abstract: Scalable quantum computing architectures increasingly rely on multi-core designs, where qubits are distributed across multiple processing cores interconnected through a quantum Network-on-Chip (NoC). In such systems, inter-core communication is typically realized through entanglement-assisted quantum teleportation, making efficient entanglement generation critical for performance. In this paper, we perform a comparative study of three entanglement management paradigms for multi-core quantum processors: reactive on-demand generation (ODG), proactive continuous pre-generation (CGP), and an adaptive continuous pre-generation approach (ACGP). While ODG generates entanglement only when required, CGP reduces average teleportation latency by pre-generating EPR pairs in the background. To improve upon this, we propose ACGP which dynamically adjusts entanglement generation probabilities based on observed inter-core communication patterns. We evaluate these approaches using an extended SeQUeNCe simulator on mesh-based multi-core architectures on real benchmark circuits. Results show that ACGP significantly reduces average teleportation latency compared to ODG and CGP. Although pre-generation introduces fidelity degradation due to storage time, entanglement purification effectively restores fidelity with minimal impact on latency. These results demonstrate that adaptive entanglement managements can substantially improve communication efficiency in scalable quantum multi-core systems.

</details>


### [113] [Emergence and Recovery of (logical) Kochen-Specker Contextuality via Hamilton Extension](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14629)
*Jayashree Karmakar,Biswadeep Chatterjee,Rafiuddin Gazi,Anushko Chattopadhyay,Ananya Chakraborty,Snehasish Roy Chowdhury,Amit Mukherjee,Manik Banik*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出Hamilton扩展方法，证明KS不可着色性可以从KS可着色的向量集通过构造性过程产生，并确定六向量是产生KS矛盾的最小父集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统观点认为Kochen-Specker (KS) 上下文性是由特殊构造的测量配置所固有的内在属性，本文试图证明KS上下文性可以从KS可着色的向量集中通过构造性过程产生，揭示上下文性产生的新结构路径。

Method: 提出Hamilton扩展方法，为四维向量集中的每个实向量关联一个测量上下文，同时在不同父向量的Hamilton扩展子代之间诱导额外的测量上下文，从而改变兼容性结构。

Result: 建立了精确且最优的阈值：每个五向量父集的Hamilton扩展仍保持KS可着色，而适当选择的六向量父集即可产生逻辑KS矛盾。因此，六向量是通过该机制产生KS矛盾的最小父集。

Conclusion: 六向量是产生KS矛盾的最小父集，结果揭示了上下文性的新结构路径，为构建紧凑KS集合提供了系统框架，对基于上下文性的量子信息协议和图论非经典性方法具有重要意义。

Abstract: Logical Kochen-Specker (KS) contextuality is widely regarded as an intrinsic property of specially constructed measurement configurations. We show instead that it can emerge from KS-colorable vector sets through a constructive procedure we call the Hamilton extension. Defined for four-dimensional vector sets, the Hamilton extension associates each real vector with a measurement context while inducing additional measurement contexts among Hamilton-extended children of distinct parent vectors. These emergent contexts fundamentally alter the compatibility structure, transforming KS-colorable configurations into KS-uncolorable ones and recovering logical contextuality lost under apex-vertex augmentation. We establish a sharp and optimal threshold -- the Hamilton extension of every five-vector parent set remains KS-colorable, whereas suitably chosen six-vector parent sets already generate logical KS contradictions. Thus, six vectors constitute the smallest parent set capable of generating KS contradiction through this mechanism. Our results reveal a new structural route to contextuality, provide a systematic framework for constructing compact KS sets, and have implications for contextuality-based quantum information protocols and graph-theoretic approaches to nonclassicality.

</details>


### [114] [Worst-Case Quantum Algorithm for Optimal Polynomial Intersection Beyond Decoded Quantum Interferometry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14650)
*Shuji Horinaga,Takashi Yamakawa*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文针对最优多项式交集（OPI）问题，提出了一种超越解码量子干涉测量（DQI）算法参数范围的量子算法，并在最坏情况下实现了此前仅平均情况下可达到的性能，同时改进了Sun和Wootters的存在性界。主要成果包括：当每个子集包含约一半域元素时，算法在速率R>0.75时达到完全满足率s=1（匹配此前平均情况界，而DQI需R=1），存在性界从R>0.7495改进至R>0.7158。这些结果推广至最大距离可分（MDS）码的Max-LINSAT问题，技术核心是MDS设置下Brascamp-Lieb型不等式的新应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: OPI问题在参数区域内，DQI量子算法表现优于经典启发式算法，但后续工作仅分析了平均情况。Sun和Wootters证明了最坏情况下OPI在更大参数区域内存在解，但未给出相应的量子算法。本文旨在设计一种在最坏情况下超越DQI参数区域的量子算法。

Method: 通过MDS码设置下Brascamp-Lieb型不等式的新应用，将存在性结果推广至Max-LINSAT问题；对于算法部分，仅适用于其对偶码具有高效列表译码器的MDS码。算法设计利用了量子计算的特性，结合新的不等式工具进行分析。

Result: 1. 当每个子集包含约一半域元素时，算法在R>0.75时达到s=1（完全满足），匹配平均情况界，而DQI仅能在R=1时实现s=1；2. 存在性界从R>0.7495改进至R>0.7158；3. 存在性结果推广至任意MDS码的Max-LINSAT问题；4. 算法结果适用于对偶码有高效列表译码器的MDS码。

Conclusion: 本文成功设计了最坏情况下超越DQI参数区域的量子算法，填补了Sun和Wootters工作留下的空白。同时改进了存在性界，并将结果推广至更一般的MDS码设置。Brascamp-Lieb型不等式的新应用可能具有更广泛的后续研究价值。

Abstract: The Optimal Polynomial Intersection (OPI) problem asks us to find a low-degree polynomial over a finite field whose values lie in prescribed subsets on as many given inputs as possible. Decoded quantum interferometry (DQI) gives a quantum algorithm for OPI in parameter regimes beyond those achieved by the best known classical heuristics. Follow-up works improve the parameter regimes, but their analyses are limited to average-case settings. Recently, Sun and Wootters showed that, even in the worst case, OPI has a solution in a larger parameter regime than the one covered by DQI. However, they left open whether one can design a quantum algorithm that solves OPI in the worst-case beyond the DQI regime.
  We give such a quantum algorithm. As a byproduct, we also improve the existential bound of Sun and Wootters in certain parameter regimes. In particular, when each subset contains roughly half of the field elements, our algorithm finds a solution with satisfaction rate $s=1$ whenever the rate satisfies $R>0.75$. This matches the previous average-case bound, whereas DQI cannot achieve $s=1$ unless $R=1$. Our existential bound guarantees the existence of a solution when $R> 0.7158$, improving over the previous threshold $R>0.7495$. More generally, our existential results extend to the Max-LINSAT problem with respect to arbitrary maximum distance separable (MDS) codes. The corresponding algorithmic results apply only to MDS codes whose dual admits an efficient list decoder. Our results are obtained through a novel application of a Brascamp--Lieb-type inequality in the MDS setting, which may have further applications.

</details>


### [115] [Tuning the universality class of a quantum process by Trotterization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14663)
*Robin Prampain dit Boulan,Jerom Jebai,Hendrik Weimer*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文数值分析了量子接触过程的离散化版本，发现其相变普适类取决于离散化细节，并通过连续插值统一了先前看似矛盾的结果，证明普适类改变源于量子相消干涉效应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先前关于量子接触过程的连续时间版本研究表明其相变属于新的量子普适类，而相关过程的实验却指向经典有向渗流类，两者存在矛盾，需要澄清差异来源。

Method: 采用数值分析方法研究量子接触过程的离散化版本，构建系统性的连续插值方法来桥接离散化细节导致的不同结果。

Result: 证明了连续时间与离散时间版本之间的差异源于离散化细节，并构建了连续插值方法统一了这些结果。

Conclusion: 普适类的修改归因于量子相消干涉效应，表明其具有明确的量子力学起源。

Abstract: We numerically analyze a discretized version of the quantum contact process, a non-equilibrium model having a competition between a coherent infection process and a dissipative recovery process. Previously, the continuous-time version has been discussed to have a phase transition in a novel quantum universality class, while experimental investigations of a related process have pointed towards the transition being in the classical directed percolation class. We demonstrate that these differences stem from details of the discretization of the process and construct a systematic way to continuously interpolate between these results. Finally, we provide evidence that the modification of the universality class is due to destructive interference effects, showing a clear quantum-mechanical origin.

</details>


### [116] [Electrons Hopping across a Molecular Network: Spectra and Symmetries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14687)
*Ludwig Schulz,Max Best,Carsten Henkel*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该论文研究了有限分子环网络中自旋无关电子的Hubbard模型，分析了晶格几何、库仑相互作用和离散对称性对多体能谱的影响，以及外部磁场下的塞曼效应。重点关注了偶奇粒子数系统的定性区别和半填充时粒子-空穴对称性对塞曼分裂的保护作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究有限分子环网络中晶格几何、库仑相互作用与离散对称性之间的相互影响，特别是网络对称性如何决定多体能谱结构和本征态的Mulliken分类。

Method: 采用紧束缚Hubbard哈密顿量描述自旋无关电子，运用群论方法分析L=3,4,5,6节点的环网络，填充1-3个电子，研究外部磁通下的能谱演化。

Result: 塞曼效应解除简并并与库仑相互作用结合导致对称扇区中的避免交叉；发现了偶数和奇数粒子数系统之间的定性区别；半填充时粒子-空穴对称性保护特定对称扇区免受塞曼分裂。

Conclusion: 粒子-空穴对称性（对偶性）在半填充条件下能够保护特定对称扇区不被塞曼效应分裂，这一发现揭示了对称性在调控量子系统能谱结构中的关键作用。

Abstract: We investigate interacting spinless electrons on finite molecular ring networks described by a tight-binding Hubbard Hamiltonian. The interplay between lattice geometry, Coulomb interactions and discrete symmetries is analysed for rings with $L=3,4,5,6$ nodes, filled with one, two or three electrons. Special attention is devoted to the role of the network symmetries in determining the structure of the many-body spectrum and the Mulliken classification of the eigenstates. Using group-theoretical methods, we examine the evolution of the spectra in the presence of an external magnetic flux. The Zeeman effect lifts degeneracies and results in combination with the Coulomb interaction to avoided crossings in symmetry sectors. We identify a qualitatively distinction between systems with an even and odd number of particles. At half-filling, particle-hole symmetry (duality) protects selected symmetry sectors against Zeeman splitting.

</details>


### [117] [High-rate continuous-variable quantum key distribution coexisting with Tb/s coherent classical transmission in hollow-core fiber](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14704)
*Xitao Ji,Siyu Chen,Peng Li,Mingming Zhang,Yilun Chen,Jun Gao,Rui Lin,Bacco Davide,Siqi Yan,Ming Tang*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文通过将超低损耗反谐振空芯光纤与残余载波辅助离散调制连续变量量子密钥分发(DM-CV-QKD)相结合，在24.3公里链路实现了153.22 Mb/s渐近密钥率和149.99 Mb/s有限码长密钥率，同时与39个相干波分复用信道共存（总数据速率7.6 Tb/s），展示了空芯光纤在量子-经典光网络深度融合中的巨大潜力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统实芯光纤中，量子信号与高容量经典信号共存时，弱量子态容易受到过量噪声干扰，限制了QKD在实际网络中的部署。

Method: 采用超低损耗反谐振空芯光纤作为传输介质，结合残余载波辅助离散调制连续变量QKD方案，解决传播引入的共存噪声和低信噪比下的相位恢复问题。

Result: 在24.3公里、3.3 dB端到端损耗的空芯链路上，15 Gbaud双极化DM-CV-QKD信道实现153.22 Mb/s渐近SKR和149.99 Mb/s有限码长SKR；39个相干WDM信道提供7.6 Tb/s总速率；即使在没有BPF、经典发射功率高达15 dBm时仍能维持正密钥率；100公里等效条件下有限码长分析仍预测正密钥率。

Conclusion: 反谐振空芯光纤结合载波辅助相位恢复可极大扩展光纤共享量子安全相干链路的工作区间，为高容量CV-QKD与高速光网络的集成提供了一条有前景的路径。

Abstract: Quantum key distribution (QKD) can provide secret keys with security rooted in quantum mechanics, but operation alongside high-capacity classical traffic remains limited by the excess-noise budget of weak quantum states in conventional solid-core fiber. Here, we combine ultralow-loss anti-resonant hollow-core fiber with residual-carrier-assisted discrete-modulation continuous-variable QKD (DM-CV-QKD) to address both propagation-induced coexistence noise and low-SNR phase recovery. Over a 24.3-km hollow-core link with 3.3-dB end-to-end loss, a dual-polarization 15-Gbaud DM-CV-QKD channel achieves an average asymptotic secret-key rate (SKR) of 153.22 Mb/s and a finite-size SKR of 149.99 Mb/s, while 39 coherent wavelength-division-multiplexed channels deliver an aggregate data rate of 7.6 Tb/s and a net data rate of 7.2 Tb/s. The system can even sustain a positive SKR under a high classical launch power of up to 15 dBm, without an optical bandpass filter (BPF). Finite-size analysis against collective attacks further yields a projected positive secret-key rate at a 100-km-equivalent condition. These results show that an anti-resonant hollow-core fiber, combined with carrier-assisted phase recovery, can greatly extend the operating regime of shared-fiber quantum-secured coherent links, pointing to a promising approach for integrating high-rate CV-QKD with high-capacity optical networks.

</details>


### [118] [Moment Optimization in the Navascués-Pironio-Acín Hierarchy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14755)
*Francesco Flora,Losel Matos,Tim Heightman,Tamás Kriváchy,Adan Garriga,Antonio Acín*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文针对NPA层次结构中算子矩选择问题，将其重构为组合子集选择问题，发现矩之间存在高阶协同交互作用。比较了并行回火、RBM强化学习策略和贝叶斯优化三种方法，在$I_{3322}$贝尔不等式基准测试中，RBM方法在困难过渡区间最接近最优解，成本仅为暴力搜索的约两个数量级以下。将该框架应用于174个贝尔不等式和一维海森堡自旋链，证明物理驱动的单项式基是可压缩的且非全局最优，预算感知搜索将长程关联的认证界改进了近两个数量级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: NPA层次结构是非交换多项式优化问题的重要方法，但其实际应用受限于算子矩数量的组合增长。由于不同矩对界紧致性的贡献不同，在固定计算预算下如何选择最优的矩子集是一个关键问题。

Method: 将矩选择重构为组合子集选择问题，利用复杂系统理论中的边际协同诊断量化矩之间的高阶协同交互作用。开发并比较了三种优化方法：并行回火(PT)、基于RBM的强化学习策略和贝叶斯优化(BO)。

Result: 三种方法均显著优于贪心方法，成本约为暴力搜索的两个数量级以下，其中RBM在$I_{3322}$的困难过渡区间最接近最优解。在174个贝尔不等式实验中观察到异质性收敛行为；在一维海森堡自旋链中，预算感知搜索将长程关联的认证界改进了近两个数量级。

Conclusion: 建立了非交换多项式优化中矩选择的可扩展框架，证明物理驱动的单项式基可被压缩且非全局最优，为量子物理和量子信息中的NPA层次结构应用提供了新的优化途径。

Abstract: The Navascués-Pironio-Acín (NPA) hierarchy provides a convergent sequence of semidefinite programming (SDP) relaxations for noncommutative polynomial optimisation, ubiquitous in quantum physics. However, its practical applicability is limited by the combinatorial growth in operator moments required at each level. Since not all moments contribute equally to bound tightness, selecting moments within a fixed computational budget is a relevant problem.
  We reframe moment selection as combinatorial subset selection and show it is governed by strong higher-order synergistic interactions among moments, quantified through a marginal synergy diagnostic adapted from complex systems theory. We develop and compare three optimisation methods: Parallel Tempering (PT), an RBM-based reinforcement learning policy, and Bayesian Optimisation (BO). On the $I_{3322}$ Bell inequality benchmark, all three substantially outperform greedy approaches at costs around two orders of magnitude below brute force, with the RBM achieving the closest approach to optimal throughout the hard transition regime.
  We apply the framework to the 174 Bell inequalities in the $(4,4,2,2)$ scenario, finding heterogeneous convergence behaviour across inequalities, and to the one-dimensional Heisenberg spin chain, demonstrating that physically motivated monomial bases are internally compressible and are not globally optimal in general. A budget-aware search over a broader pool improves certified bounds on long-range correlations by nearly two orders of magnitude. These results establish a scalable framework for moment selection in noncommutative polynomial optimisation, with broad applications across quantum physics and quantum information.

</details>


### [119] [Robustness of periodicity in Grover walks under a magnetic vector potential](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14797)
*Hiroto Sekido,Etsuo Segawa*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究磁场矢量势对有限图上周期性格罗弗游走的影响，发现周期性的鲁棒性依赖于图的谱结构，并推导出描述扰动态的厄米矩阵，最终表明该动态渐近地等价于由该厄米矩阵驱动的连续时间量子游走。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究磁场矢量势作为扰动时，周期性格罗弗游走周期性的鲁棒性，并揭示其与底层图谱结构的关系。

Method: 通过量子图框架引入磁矢量势（格罗弗游走为其特例），将矢量势视为周期性格罗弗游走的扰动；当图存在至少一个非简单特征值时，推导出一个刻画周期性鲁棒性的厄米矩阵。

Result: 周期性的鲁棒性依赖于图的谱结构；当图存在非简单特征值时，存在一个厄米矩阵表征其鲁棒性；扰动后的动力学渐近地由该厄米矩阵生成的连续时间量子游走描述。

Conclusion: 磁场矢量势对周期性格罗弗游走的影响可通过一个厄米矩阵刻画，扰动态在渐近意义下等价于连续时间量子游走。

Abstract: We study the effect of magnetic vector potentials on periodic Grover walks on finite graphs. The magnetic vector potential is introduced through the framework of quantum graphs, which induces the Grover walk as a special case. We regard the vector potential as a perturbation of a periodic Grover walk and investigate the robustness of its periodicity. Our analysis reveals that the response to such perturbations depends on the spectral structure of the underlying graph. In particular, when the graph possesses at least one non-simple eigenvalue, we derive a Hermitian matrix that characterizes the robustness of its periodicity. As a consequence, we show that the perturbed dynamics is asymptotically described by a continuous-time quantum walk generated by this Hermitian matrix.

</details>


### [120] [Quantum Remote Implementation of Hybrid Operations on Hyperstates Using Hyperentangled States](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14819)
*Satish Kumar,Anirban Pathak*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出一种利用双光子双自由度超纠缠态同时操控极化和空间模式来远程实现任意混合算符的量子远程控制协议，并分析了测量误差和耗散对成功概率的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有QRIO协议大多使用超纠缠态但每次仅利用一个自由度，未能同时利用多个自由度进行远程操作，限制了效率和能力。

Method: 利用光子的极化和空间模式构建双量子比特超纠缠态作为共享资源，采用线性光学元件和交叉Kerr非线性相互作用实现光子-光子耦合，从而同时利用两个自由度远程实现任意混合算符。

Result: 分析了有限相干态可区分性和相干态耗散导致的测量误差影响，并评估了协议的成功概率。适当选择交叉Kerr相移和相干态振幅可显著提升协议性能。

Conclusion: 该方案能够有效提升量子远程控制的性能，是混合量子通信和分布式量子信息处理的有前途的候选方案。

Abstract: Quantum remote control, also known as quantum remote implementation of an operator (QRIO), enables the remote manipulation of an arbitrary quantum state by implementing a desired quantum operation at a distant location. Significant progress has recently been made in developing QRIO protocols and their variants. Most existing schemes employ hyperentangled states where entanglement is shared across multiple degrees of freedom (DOFs). However, these protocols typically exploit only one degree of freedom at a time. In this work, we propose a QRIO protocol that simultaneously utilizes the polarization and spatial DOFs of a two-qubit hyperentangled state to remotely implement an arbitrary hybrid operator on an unknown single-photon two-qubit hyperstate. The shared hyperentangled resource is realized using the polarization and spatial modes of photons, while the protocol is constructed using linear optical elements and cross-Kerr nonlinear interactions to facilitate effective photon-photon coupling. Furthermore, the effects of measurement errors arising from finite coherent state distinguishability and coherent state dissipation are analyzed and the corresponding success probability of the protocol is evaluated. The results demonstrate that an appropriate choice of the cross-Kerr phase shift and coherent state amplitude significantly enhances the protocol performance, making the proposed scheme a promising candidate for hybrid quantum communication and distributed quantum information processing.

</details>


### [121] [Binary Gauss Stabilizers for Abelian Lattice Gauge Theories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14861)
*Matteo Turco,Luca Spagnoli,Alessandro Roggero*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文揭示了规范理论与量子纠错码的深层联系，针对Z_N（N为2的幂）离散阿贝尔格点规范理论，提出了“二元高斯稳定子”这一替代方案，用于描述规范不变子空间，并基于此构建了无需额外量子比特的纠错码，同时提供了一种新的规范固定策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索规范理论与量子纠错码之间的内在结构对应关系，将规范理论的约束（高斯定律）类比为量子纠错中的稳定子，并利用这一联系开发实用的量子纠错码以及新的规范固定方法。

Method: 针对Z_N（N为2的幂）离散阿贝尔格点规范理论，提出了二元高斯稳定子（binary Gauss stabilizers）作为规范不变子空间的稳定子集，替代传统的高斯算符；基于此稳定子群构建纠错码，并设计了新的规范固定策略。

Result: 成功构建了基于规范对称性的纠错码，无需额外量子比特；同时提出了不同于轴向规范等已有方法的新规范固定策略。

Conclusion: 该工作提供了研究格点规范理论及其量子模拟的新工具，为规范理论与量子信息交叉领域的未来研究开辟了方向。

Abstract: Gauge theories and quantum error-correcting codes share the same underlying structure: both use constraints to identify a specific subspace of the full Hilbert space. In quantum error correction, these constraints are known as stabilizers, while in gauge theories they correspond to Gauss law. In this work, we consider a family of discrete Abelian lattice gauge theories described by a $\mathbb{Z}_{N}$ gauge group with $N$ an arbitrary power of two. In this setting, we find a set of stabilizers for the gauge-invariant subspace which is an alternative to the Gauss operators, and we call them binary Gauss stabilizers. We use this alternative stabilizer group to build practical error-correcting codes exploiting the gauge symmetries of the system without the addition of extra qubits. The applications of our finding are not limited to error correction though. We also provide a new strategy of gauge fixing to remove the redundancies based on our alternative stabilizer, which might provide advantages with respect to already-existing approaches such as the axial gauge. Our results provide new tools to study lattice gauge theories and their quantum simulation, and opens directions for future work at the interface of lattice gauge theory and quantum information.

</details>


### [122] [Limits on Broadcasting Genuine Multipartite Entanglement in Quantum Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14864)
*Pritam Roy,William John Munro,Shashank Gupta*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了量子网络中广播真正多体纠缠(GME)的操作极限，证明在最优1→2克隆协议下，广播保真度随系统规模呈指数衰减，并揭示了广播副本无法同时被认证为真正多体纠缠的no-go结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索量子网络中多体纠缠广播的基本限制，特别是广播保真度与系统规模的关系，以及广播后纠缠可认证性的理论边界。

Method: 采用分布式协议，每个参与方通过分束器相互作用实现最优1→2克隆；对GHZ态、W态和Cluster态推导保真度精确表达式；利用保真度基见证框架分析GME认证可行性。

Result: 保真度随系统大小呈指数衰减：[c(R)]^N；所有态族共享源于独立后选择概率的通用归一化因子；证明两个广播副本无法同时通过保真度基见证认证为GME；在三体和四体Cluster态上验证了no-go结果的普适性。

Conclusion: 揭示了多体纠缠的可广播性与可认证性之间存在根本性权衡，刻画了量子网络中纠缠分布的内在极限，其根本障碍源于多体相干性的重新分布既降低了可达保真度又提高了认证阈值。

Abstract: We establish operational limits on the broadcasting of genuine multipartite entanglement (GME) in quantum networks. Using a distributed protocol in which each of N parties locally implements optimal 1 $\to$ 2 cloning via beam-splitter interactions, we derive exact expressions for the broadcast fidelity of Greenberger-Horne-Zeilinger (GHZ), W states, and Cluster states in a limited setting. We show that the fidelity decays exponentially with system size as [c(R)]$^N$, providing a quantitative expression of multipartite entanglement monogamy in the broadcasting setting, and that both state families share a universal normalisation factor arising from independent post-selection probabilities. Most significantly, we prove a no-go result for simultaneous GME certification: for all reflectivities and all system sizes, the two broadcast copies cannot be simultaneously certified as genuinely multipartite entangled within the standard framework of fidelity-based witnesses. We further check this behaviour for three- and four-party cluster states, finding consistent results that support the generality of the no-go beyond the GHZ and W families. This obstruction arises from the redistribution of multipartite coherence, which both reduces the achievable fidelity and increases the corresponding certification threshold. Our results reveal a fundamental trade-off between the broadcastability of multipartite entanglement and its operational certifiability, and delineate intrinsic limits on entanglement distribution in quantum networks.

</details>


### [123] [A Geometric Theory of Fermion-to-Qubit Encodings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14883)
*Lakshya Nagpal,Nishith Reen,S. R. Hassan*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文证明费米子到量子比特的映射不仅是算法工具，还定义了具有物理意义的几何表示。基于Bravyi-Kitaev和Xia-Bian-Kais编码，建立了加权超图和耦合空间表示的几何框架，引入了几何可观测量，发现了两个几何普适类及精确谱组织，并通过最优传输量化相互作用驱动的重组。在多个模型上验证了该几何描述能一致地捕捉量子多体哈密顿量的结构演化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统观点将费米子到量子比特的映射视为用于量子模拟的算法工具，本文旨在揭示这些映射同时也定义了内在的几何表示，其结构编码了超越谱等价的物理信息，从而建立超图几何作为理解量子多体哈密顿量的新框架。

Method: 基于加权超图和耦合空间表示构建几何框架；在BK表示中引入几何可观测量比较动能和相互作用超图的代数连通性，推导其对相互作用强度的精确解析依赖关系；在XBK表示中通过耦合空间的概率测度描述编码哈密顿量的演化，利用最优输运量化相互作用驱动的重组。

Result: 发现了两个几何普适类及源于编码二叉树架构的精确谱组织；在Hubbard、自旋less tV、单杂质Anderson和Kitaev模型上验证了连通性和输运的几何描述能一致地捕捉不同多体系统中编码量子哈密顿量的结构演化。

Conclusion: 费米子到量子比特的编码不仅是计算映射，还是量子多体哈密顿量的几何表示，超图几何为理解这些编码提供了新框架。

Abstract: Exact fermion to qubit transformations are conventionally regarded as algorithmic tools that translate many-body Hamiltonians into qubit representations for quantum simulation. Here we show that they also define intrinsic geometric representations whose structure encodes physically meaningful information beyond spectral equivalence. We develop a geometric framework based on weighted hypergraphs and coupling space representations constructed from the Bravyi--Kitaev (BK) and Xia--Bian--Kais (XBK) encodings. Within the BK representation, we introduce a geometric observable that compares the algebraic connectivities of the kinetic and interaction hypergraphs, derive its exact analytical dependence on interaction strength, and uncover two geometric universality classes together with an exact spectral organization originating from the binary tree architecture of the encoding. The complementary XBK representation describes the evolution of encoded Hamiltonians through probability measures in coupling space, where optimal transport quantifies interaction-driven reorganization independently of the spectral analysis. Applications to the Hubbard, spinless tV , single impurity Anderson, and Kitaev models demonstrate that these connectivity and transport based geometric descriptions consistently capture the structural evolution of encoded quantum Hamiltonians across distinct classes of many-body systems. Our results establish hypergraph geometry as a new framework for understanding fermion-to-qubit encodings,revealing that they serve not only as computational mappings but also as geometric representations of quantum many-body Hamiltonians.

</details>


### [124] [Evolution-Level Quantum Optimal Control of Single-Qubit Gates with Physics-Informed Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14884)
*Yao Du,Jian-Jian Cheng,Lin Zhang,Ming-Liang Hu,Xingang Wang*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文利用物理信息神经网络（PINN）在演化层面表示单量子比特门设计，将控制场、布洛赫态轨迹和总时长联合优化，使得优化对象从脉冲振幅转变为可微分、可诊断、可局部精炼的物理过程。该方法不仅合成门，还使量子控制具有物理可读性、可诊断性和局部可优化性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的量子门设计通常表示为脉冲优化（即优化脉冲振幅），但实际实现门的是脉冲驱动的完整受控演化。作者希望将优化对象从脉冲层面提升到演化过程层面，使量子控制不仅可合成，还具有物理可解释性和可诊断性，以更好地适应硬件特定和实验约束。

Method: 使用物理信息神经网络（PINN）表示单量子比特门设计，在布洛赫方程的约束下，联合学习控制场、布洛赫态轨迹和总时长。优化对象从脉冲振幅变为可微分的物理过程（即完整的受控演化）。

Result: 对于旋转门，优化后的演化恢复了有界单量子比特控制的预期物理组织方式，无需预设脉冲形式或扫描时长。对于几何门，该方法能识别维持几何条件的局部瓶颈，将诊断结果反馈到优化中，在保持高保真度的同时降低残余路径误差。

Conclusion: 物理信息学习不仅可用于合成量子门，还能使优化的量子控制具有物理可读性、可诊断性和局部可精炼性。这种过程级视角对适应硬件特定、任务特定和局部变化的实验约束尤为有用。

Abstract: Quantum gate design is often represented as pulse optimization, although the physical object that implements a gate is the full controlled evolution generated by the pulse. Here we use physics-informed neural networks to represent single-qubit gate design at this evolution level: the control fields, the Bloch-state trajectories, and the total duration are learned together under the Bloch equation. This changes the optimized object from pulse amplitudes to a differentiable physical process whose structure can be inspected and refined. For rotation gates, the optimized evolutions recover the physical organization expected for bounded single-qubit control, with no prescribed pulse ansatz or duration scan. For a geometric gate, the representation identifies localized bottlenecks in maintaining the geometric condition and turns this diagnosis into feedback, reducing the residual path error while preserving high fidelity. Thus physics-informed learning is used not only to synthesize gates, but also to make optimized quantum controls physically readable, diagnosable, and locally refinable. This process-level view may be especially useful for adapting gates to hardware-specific, task-specific, and locally varying experimental constraints.

</details>


### [125] [A Three-Point Continuous-Variable Quantum MacWilliams Identity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14920)
*Yinzi Xiao*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文构建了连续变量量子MacWilliams恒等式的三点形式，并证明其不优于现有两点界限，揭示了量子纠错码中投影算子的特殊性质导致三点改进失效。


<details>
