  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将经典MacWilliams恒等式从两点扩展到三点框架，探索连续变量量子纠错码的更优界限。

Method: 构造三点连续变量量子MacWilliams恒等式及其闭式积分核；利用辛不变量编码GKP量子化条件与三点符号相位；通过半定规划和坍缩定理分析量子纠错码的维度界限；采用完全正定重构绕过正定性障碍。

Result: 三点框架不优于两点界限；GKP格码的三点最优值恒等于两点线性规划最优值；E8和Leech魔函数饱和而非超越该界限；一般玻色子码中三点项因相位符号条件与Choi正定性而消失；对于单模前八个Laguerre能级的径向Choi形式验证了坍缩。

Conclusion: 量子码投影算子的定向作用虽使界限正确，但同时也消除了经典三点改进所依赖的完全正定性，导致三点推广在量子场景下无效。

Abstract: We construct the three-point continuous-variable (CV) quantum MacWilliams identity, extending the two-point framework of Burchards, and give its closed-form integral kernel. Its configuration space carries a symplectic invariant with no classical counterpart, which encodes the GKP quantization condition and a three-point sign phase. Using the identity, we derive the semidefinite-programming bounds it supports on the dimension of CV quantum error-correcting codes, and we prove, in two collapse theorems, that the three-point apparatus does not improve on the two-point bound. For GKP lattice codes the three-point optimum equals the Burchards two-point linear-programming optimum identically. This is an exact determination of the lattice three-point optimum, so the $E_8$ and Leech magic functions saturate it rather than beat it. For general bosonic codes a completely-positive reformulation bypasses the positivity obstruction that rules out the natural factored-form constructions; the phase-sign condition together with Choi positivity then force the three-point term to vanish. We certify this collapse for radial Choi forms on the first eight Laguerre levels at one mode, and leave the full trace-class cone open. Both collapses have a single cause with no classical analogue, the code projector: it orients the bound correctly but also removes the full positivity that powers the classical three-point improvement.

</details>


### [126] [Strong $O$-valued contextuality: ruling out discrete nondeterministic alternatives to quantum theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14931)
*Ravishankar Ramanathan*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文通过构造有限测量配置，将Kochen-Specker定理推广到任意有限子集O⊂[0,1]上的非确定性非上下文模型，并建立了强O值上下文性的层论框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Gleason定理通过无限连续投影格上的非上下文性识别出Born规则，但其推论Kochen-Specker定理仅排除了确定性({0,1}值)非上下文模型。本文旨在将KS定理推广到更一般的有限值非确定性情形，并建立相应的层论框架。

Method: (i) 构造一族实验可行的态依赖Hardy型测试；(ii) 证明广义KS定理，涵盖O的广泛类别并包含先前结果为特例；(iii) 在Abramsky-Brandenburger层论框架中引入强O值上下文性概念。

Result: 证明了量子理论规避所有有限值预层的全局截面；建立了概率-可能性-强上下文性的层级中关于O值的新扩展；构造了可实验检验的有限测量配置。

Conclusion: 该结果对有限多值逻辑作为量子理论的可行本体论模型具有否定性含义，并对基于上下文性的(半)设备无关协议有重要启示。

Abstract: Gleason's theorem identifies the Born rule via non-contextuality over an infinite continuous lattice of projectors, while its corollary the Kochen-Specker (KS) theorem rules out the specific class of deterministic ($\{0,1\}$-valued) noncontextual models using finite sets of projectors. Gleason's theorem also indicates the existence of KS-type finite vector constructions to rule out other discrete nondeterministic alternatives to quantum theory beyond the $\{0, 1\}$ case. Here, we construct finite measurement configurations to rule out noncontextual empirical models with outcome probabilities drawn from an arbitrary finite subset $O \subset [0,1]$. We do this by two means: (i) constructing a family of experimentally feasible state-dependent Hardy-type tests, and (ii) proving a generalized KS theorem for a broad class of $O$ that includes prior results as special cases. In the sheaf-theoretic framework of Abramsky and Brandenburger, a hierarchy of probabilistic-possibilistic-strong contextuality has been established quantifying contextuality as a resource. We extend this framework by introducing strong $O$-valued contextuality, showing that quantum theory evades global sections of all finite-valued presheaves. We also discuss the implications of the result on finite many-valued logics as viable ontological models for quantum theory and for contextuality-based (semi)-device-independent protocols.

</details>


### [127] [Pareto-optimal work extraction and the thermodynamic cost of precision in quantum information engines](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14973)
*Jonas Berx,Ted Olander,Henning Kirchberg*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了一种有限时间量子信息引擎，其中二能级系统由量子谐振子作为计量器测量，并根据测量结果有条件地提取有用功。通过多目标优化，发现了可提取功与其波动之间的帕累托最优权衡，并表明减少波动会导致更高的热力学成本：信息消耗增加、引擎循环次数增多、运行时间延长、平均功输出减少。在高精度计量器极限下，解析推导了功的分布、矩和帕累托前沿。该引擎的工作统计量退化为与单一热浴接触的量子比特。进一步分析了互信息和Fisher信息，发现帕累托最优设计接近后者相对于设备运行时间的局部极大值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子信息引擎中可提取功与功的波动之间存在基本权衡，但缺乏对这一权衡的定量描述和优化框架。

Method: 采用多目标优化方法，研究由量子谐振子作为计量器测量二能级系统的有限时间量子信息引擎；在高精度计量器极限下解析求解功的分布、矩和帕累托前沿；利用互信息和Fisher信息分析信息流。

Result: 发现了可提取功与其波动之间的帕累托最优权衡关系；减少波动会增加信息消耗、引擎循环次数、运行时间并降低平均功输出；高精度计量器极限下引擎统计量退化为与单一热浴接触的量子比特；帕累托最优设计接近Fisher信息相对于运行时间的局部极大值。

Conclusion: 该工作为量子信息引擎中功、功的波动与热力学成本之间的权衡提供了紧凑描述。

Abstract: We study a finite-time quantum information engine in which a two-level system is measured by a quantum harmonic oscillator acting as a meter and where useful work is extracted conditionally on the measurement outcome. Using multi-objective optimisation, we find a Pareto-optimal trade-off between extractable work and its fluctuations and show that reducing fluctuations entails higher thermodynamic costs: greater information consumption, more engine cycles, longer operation time, and reduced average work output. In the limit of a highly accurate meter, we obtain the work distribution, its moments, and the Pareto front analytically. In this regime, the work statistics of the engine reduce to those of a qubit in contact with a single thermal bath. We further analyse the associated information flows by examining the mutual information and Fisher information, and show that the Pareto-optimal engine designs lie very close to local maxima of the latter with respect to the operation time of the device. Our results provide a compact description of the trade-offs between work, its fluctuations, and thermodynamic costs in quantum information engines.

</details>


### [128] [Machine-Learning-Empowered Quantum Sensing of the Plaquette Phase in a Three-Level Delta System](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15040)
*Lorenzo Vitale,Shreyasi Mukherjee,Dario Fasone,Enrico Martello,Elisabetta Paladino,Luigi Giannelli,Giuseppe Falci*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种结合机器学习与相干控制的量子传感方法，利用多层感知器（MLP）通过STIRAP布居转移效率来精确估计Δ型三能级系统中的plaquette相位，为合成规范场等量子技术提供了新途径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Plaquette相位是Δ型三能级系统中一种规范不变的重要物理量，深刻影响系统动力学（如破坏相干布居俘获），但缺乏有效的实验可测方法来直接识别该相位。

Method: 采用监督学习的多层感知器（MLP），以不同驱动条件下STIRAP过程的布居转移效率作为输入特征，训练网络对plaquette相位进行估计。

Result: MLP能够从实验可观测的STIRAP布居转移效率中准确估计plaquette相位，展示了机器学习与相干控制结合的有效性。

Conclusion: 该方法实现了闭环量子系统中规范不变量的有效相位识别，为量子传感特别是合成规范场相关的量子技术应用开辟了新前景。

Abstract: We propose a machine-learning-empowered approach to the quantum sensing of the plaquette phase, a gauge-invariant quantity arising in three-level $Δ$ systems. This phase profoundly affects the system dynamics, breaking coherent population trapping and inducing a non-trivial phase dependence of the dynamics. We demonstrate that a multi-layer perceptron (MLP), trained in a supervised-learning framework, can accurately estimate the plaquette phase from STImulated Raman Adiabatic Passage (STIRAP) population transfer efficiencies measured under different driving conditions, which provide experimentally accessible observables. Our results highlight how the combination of coherent control and machine learning (ML) enables effective phase identification in closed-loop quantum systems, opening new perspectives for quantum technologies, specifically quantum sensing applications including synthetic gauge fields.

</details>


### [129] [Towards logical entanglement creation in trivalent planar architectures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15044)
*Lukas Bödeker,Luis Colmenarez,Sergey Blinov,Ants Remm,Simon Gustavsson,Markus Müller*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了三价（三连通）架构下的表面码晶格手术方案，与传统的四价测量方案相比，在总量子比特数O(d²)中节省O(d)个量子比特，在总双量子比特门数O(d³)中节省O(d)个双量子比特门，并在距离为三时逻辑保真度提升约25%，为可扩展平面三价量子比特架构实现基于表面码的逻辑量子处理器开辟了道路。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 低开销的量子纠错方案对于在多逻辑量子比特寄存器上实现量子计算至关重要。在平面架构中，表面码已成为主流范式，而近期理论和实验工作表明物理量子比特连接度为三即可实现容错量子纠错。因此需要在三价架构下研究晶格手术方案。

Method: 本研究在三价（trivalent）架构背景下研究晶格手术（lattice surgery），引入可扩展的电路构造来实现该方案，并与四价测量方案进行对比分析。同时基于fluxonium量子比特架构进行实验模拟，基准测试两种晶格手术方案的逻辑保真度。

Result: 三价晶格手术协议相比四价测量方案，在总量子比特数O(d²)中节省O(d)个量子比特，在总双量子比特门数O(d³)中节省O(d)个双量子比特门；针对距离为三的架构，逻辑保真度提升约25%。

Conclusion: 这些结果为可扩展的平面三价量子比特架构承载基于表面码的逻辑量子处理器开辟了道路。

Abstract: Low-overhead quantum error-correction schemes are essential for enabling quantum computation on registers containing multiple logical qubits. For planar architectures with limited nearest-neighbor qubit connectivity, the surface code has emerged as the leading paradigm. Recent theoretical and experimental work has shown that a physical-qubit connectivity of degree three is sufficient to implement fault-tolerant quantum error correction. In this work, we study lattice surgery in the context of such trivalent architectures and introduce scalable circuit constructions to implement it. Compared with the four-valent measurement scheme, the trivalent lattice-surgery protocol reduces the required resources by $\mathcal{O}(d)$ qubits out of a total qubit count of $\mathcal{O}(d^2)$ and by $\mathcal{O}(d)$ two-qubit gates out of a total two-qubit gate count of $\mathcal{O}(d^3)$. We benchmark the logical fidelity of both lattice-surgery schemes in terms of experimentally realistic simulations targeting an implementation with a fluxonium qubit based architecture and find a potential improvement of up to $\approx25\%$ for distance-three. These results open a way for scalable planar trivalent qubit architectures to host a surface-code-based logical quantum processor.

</details>


### [130] [SQD-Enabled Circuit Compression for Resource-Efficient Quantum Chemistry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15076)
*Kangyu Zheng,Yidong Zhou,Jinglei Cheng,Zhemin Zhang,Shaohua Li,Zhiding Liang*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文通过梯度算符剪枝和Clifford舍入两种压缩技术，揭示了子空间量子对角化(SQD)方法对量子采样电路表达能力的鲁棒性：即使去除50%的非Clifford操作和变分表达能力，SQD仍能保持化学精度内的基态能量估计，并实现高达33倍的模拟加速和2.8倍的硬件电路深度缩减。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索SQD方法中未被充分研究的鲁棒性特性——即采样电路可以去除多少非Clifford操作和变分表达能力而仍保持SQD精度不退化。

Method: 提出两种互补的压缩技术：(1) 梯度算符剪枝，丢弃低影响的激发算符；(2) Clifford舍入，将剩余参数摄动到最近的Clifford角度。两者应用于VQE拟设的量子位约化哈密顿量上。

Result: 在21个分子的系统消融研究中，即使两个轴均达到50%压缩，SQD中位误差仍保持在化学精度内，模拟速度提升达33倍。在6个分子的IBM量子硬件验证中，实现了高达2.8倍的编译深度缩减，且SQD精度无损失。

Conclusion: SQD对采样电路的表达能力具有显著的鲁棒性，可以通过大幅压缩电路中的非Clifford操作和变分参数来提升计算效率，而不牺牲最终能量估计的准确性。

Abstract: Subspace Quantum Diagonalization (SQD) recovers ground-state energies by classically diagonalizing a Hamiltonian in the subspace spanned by quantum samples, requiring only bitstrings with sufficient ground-state overlap rather than an accurate variational energy. We reveal and exploit this underexplored robustness property: how much non-Clifford and variational expressivity can be removed from the sampling circuit before SQD accuracy degrades? We answer through two complementary compression techniques: gradient-based operator pruning, which discards low-impact excitation operators, and Clifford rounding, which snaps remaining parameters to the nearest Clifford angle. Both of these techniques can be applied to a VQE ansatz on a qubit-reduced Hamiltonian. A systematic ablation study across 21 molecules shows that median SQD error stays within chemical accuracy even at 50\% compression on both axes, while simulation speedup reaches $33\times$. Hardware validation on 6 molecules on IBM quantum hardware confirms up to $2.8\times$ transpiled-depth reduction with zero loss in SQD accuracy.

</details>


### [131] [An experimental pathway towards an exact theory of strong coupling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15089)
*Eugenia Pyurbeeva,Ronnie Kosloff*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文利用数学等价形式的GKLS主方程，对与环境强耦合的二能级系统进行了精确理论描述，并提出了基于固态电子量子点器件的实验方案来揭示强耦合的基本参数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强耦合区域中描述系统的关键参数（如详细平衡关系的类比和两个耦合强度常数）在弱耦合框架之外是未知的，需要发展新的理论和实验方法来揭示这些参数，从而深化对强耦合物理的理解。

Method: 采用数学上等价于GKLS主方程的形式，建立二能级系统与环境强耦合的精确理论描述；提出基于固态电子量子点器件的可行实验协议。

Result: 该框架直观且揭示了问题物理本质，与现有结果（如热化到非正则态）一致，但存在三个在弱耦合区域外未知的参数。

Conclusion: 通过所提出的量子点实验协议可以揭示强耦合的基本参数，从而推动对强耦合物理的基本理解。

Abstract: We employ a mathematically equivalent form of the GKLS master equation to arrive at an exact theoretical description of a two-level system strongly coupled to the environment. The framework, while intuitive, shedding light on the physics of the problem, and agreeing with existing results, such as thermalisation to a non-canonical state, is based around three parameters that are unknown outside of the weak coupling regime -- the analogue to the detailed balance relation, and two coupling strength constants. As a way forward, we propose a feasible experimental protocol based on a solid-state electronic quantum dot device, through which the fundamental parameters of the problem can be revealed, which would further the fundamental understanding of strong coupling.

</details>


### [132] [Quantum Lock-In Detection via Successive Adiabatic Evolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15121)
*Kangze Li,Siqi Chen,Hao Zhang,Jiazhao Tian,Liantuan Xiao,Gerardo Adesso*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于连续量子绝热演化的量子锁相检测通用协议，通过三角波调制从根本上解决了频谱泄漏问题，并在金刚石氮-空位中心系统中展示了其对弱交变信号的鲁棒检测能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有量子锁相检测协议中，脉冲形式的控制操作导致严重的频谱泄漏，限制了信噪比和弱信号检测精度。

Method: 采用连续量子绝热演化代替脉冲控制，通过绝热控制量子探针的时间演化实现三角波调制函数，从而消除频谱泄漏并提取目标信号的完整特征。

Result: 在金刚石氮-空位中心系统中实现了绝热量子锁相检测方案，证明了该协议对实验缺陷具有强鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 绝热量子锁相检测是一种鲁棒且实验可行的弱交变信号检测方法，可推动实际量子传感技术的发展。

Abstract: In recent years, quantum lock-in detection has emerged as a promising technique to accurately detect weak signals submerged in background noise. However, the signal-to-noise ratio of existing protocols is severely limited by spectral leakage resulting from control operations implemented in pulse form. Here, we propose a general protocol for realizing quantum lock-in detection by employing successive quantum adiabatic evolution. In our protocol, the signal modulation is achieved by adiabatically controlling the time evolution of the quantum probe, which enables the implementation of triangular modulation functions. The realization of triangular-wave modulation fundamentally solves the problem of spectral leakage and facilitates the extraction of the complete characteristics of the target signals. We present a practical implementation scheme of adiabatic quantum lock-in detection based on nitrogen-vacancy centers in diamond, and demonstrate that the proposed protocol possesses strong resilience against experimental imperfections. Our results establish adiabatic quantum lock-in detection as a robust and experimentally accessible approach to detection of weak alternating signals in noisy environments, thus promoting the advance of real-world quantum sensing technologies.

</details>


### [133] [On the origin of finite entanglement scaling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15124)
*Luke Hodgkiss,Laurens Lootens,Atsushi Ueda,Bram Vanhecke,Frank Verstraete*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文解决了有限纠缠标度理论中的开放问题，揭示了矩阵乘积态近似临界系统时产生的实际扰动与共形场论预测存在显著差异，并为此开发了一种稀疏线性求解器，可隐式计算二维张量网络对定义参数的导数，该算法对变分优化投影纠缠对态具有独立价值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决有限纠缠标度理论中一个悬而未决的问题：确定矩阵乘积态近似临界系统时实际诱导的扰动，并验证其与共形场论预测的一致性。

Method: 开发了一种稀疏线性求解器，以隐式方式计算二维张量网络对其定义参数的前向和后向导数。

Result: 发现矩阵乘积态近似临界系统时产生的实际扰动与共形场论预测的扰动存在显著差异。

Conclusion: 有限纠缠标度理论中实际扰动模式与共形场论预测不同；所提出的稀疏线性求解器可作为变分优化投影纠缠对态的原始工具，规避传统自动微分方法的不稳定性问题。

Abstract: The concept of finite entanglement scaling forms one of the pillars on which the tensor network ecosystem is built. In this paper, we resolve the open problem of determining the actual perturbations induced by matrix product state approximations of critical systems, and we demonstrate that these can be quite different than the ones predicted by conformal field theory. To that aim, we develop a sparse linear solver to calculate the forward and backward derivatives of 2-dimensional tensor networks with respect to their defining parameters in an implicit way. This algorithm is of independent interest as it provides a primitive for the variational optimization of projected entangled pair states that circumvents the instabilities plaguing traditional automatic differentiation methods.

</details>


### [134] [Periodic orbits and quantum many-body scars in integrable spin chains](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15132)
*Elena Petrova,Maksym Serbyn,Marko Ljubotina*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文利用周期轨道框架研究可积XYZ/XXZ自旋-1/2链中的量子多体疤痕，通过Bethe-ansatz量子数识别疤痕轨道及其支撑本征态，揭示了由零动量磁振子对修饰的公共弦芯结构，并展示了即使在可积性被横向场破缺时该结构仍持续存在。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子多体疤痕是热化动力学中的例外，但其与可积性的关系尚不明确。本文旨在通过周期轨道框架和Bethe-ansatz方法，建立量子疤痕的周期轨道图像与可积模型代数结构之间的直接联系。

Method: 将周期轨道框架应用于可积XYZ/XXZ自旋-1/2链，发展能量分辨方法以定位和追踪跨能谱的周期轨道；利用Bethe-ansatz量子数识别疤痕轨道及其支撑本征态；通过分析Bethe方程中零动量磁振子对的退耦，解析推导出等距塔间距；并通过横向场破缺可积性验证结构的鲁棒性。

Result: 识别出由零动量磁振子对修饰的公共弦芯结构的疤痕轨道族；在Bethe-ansatz框架内直接重构疤痕本征态及相关塔结构；解析解释了等距塔间距；证明该结构在可积性破缺后仍持续存在。

Conclusion: 建立了量子疤痕的周期轨道图像与可积模型代数结构之间的直接联系，表明疤痕动力学的关键特征可以在Bethe-ansatz框架内得到解析理解。

Abstract: Quantum many-body scars provide an exception to generic thermalising dynamics, but their relation to integrability remains unclear. Here we apply the periodic-orbit framework to the integrable XYZ/XXZ spin-1/2 chain, developing an energy-resolved approach that enables us to locate and track periodic orbits across the spectrum. We identify families of scarred orbits and resolve their supporting eigenstates in terms of Bethe-ansatz quantum numbers, revealing a common string core dressed by zero-momentum magnon pairs. This allows us to reconstruct both the scarred eigenstates and the associated towers directly within the Bethe ansatz, and explain the equidistant tower spacing analytically from the decoupling of zero-momentum magnon pairs in the Bethe equations, providing a microscopic realisation of scar phenomenology in an integrable setting. We further show that this structure persists upon breaking integrability with a transverse field, with the periodic orbits continuing to govern the dynamics. Our results establish a direct connection between the periodic-orbit picture of quantum scarring and the algebraic structure of integrable models, showing that key features of scar dynamics can be understood analytically within the Bethe ansatz framework.

</details>


### [135] [Superadditivity for Entanglement-Assisted Communication](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15151)
*Hao-Chung Cheng,Mario Berta*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文证明即使纠缠辅助容量是可加性的，但Petz-Rényi信道信息在α∈[1/2,1)范围内存在严格超可加性，这意味着跨信道使用的联合编码可以增强纠缠辅助通信的可靠性（误差指数），即使不能提高通信速率。该现象在测量信道（纠缠破坏信道）中即可实现，且不需要传输系统之间的纠缠。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 突破传统认知：虽然纠缠辅助容量是单字母可加的（即联合编码不能提高通信速率），但需要探究这种可加性是否延伸到通信可靠性（误差指数）领域。

Method: 通过分析Petz-Rényi信道信息的超可加性，在测量信道（纠缠破坏信道）中构造严格超可加性的解析证明，使用可分离的经典关联的二拷贝信道输入边际分布作为例子。

Result: 1) Petz-Rényi信道信息在α∈[1/2,1)范围内严格超可加；2) 纠缠辅助随机编码的误差指数存在真正的多拷贝增强；3) 该现象在测量信道（纠缠破坏、无辅助容量可加）中即可实现；4) 只需要可分离的经典关联输入，无需系统间纠缠。

Conclusion: 跨信道使用的关联虽然不能提高纠缠辅助通信的极限速率，但可以增强其可靠性（降低错误概率）。

Abstract: The entanglement-assisted capacity of a quantum channel admits an additive single-letter characterization, implying that joint encodings across channel uses cannot increase the ultimate communication rate. Here, we show that this additive picture does not extend to communication reliability. Specifically, we prove that the Petz-Rényi channel information can be strictly superadditive for every $α\in[1/2,1)$, yielding a genuine multi-copy enhancement of the entanglement-assisted random-coding error exponent, even though the entanglement-assisted capacity remains additive. We establish this phenomenon analytically already for measurement channels, which are entanglement-breaking and have additive unassisted capacity. Remarkably, this strict superadditivity is witnessed by a separable, classically correlated two-copy channel-input marginal, demonstrating that no entanglement between the transmitted systems is required. Our results show that, although correlations across channel uses cannot increase the ultimate rate of entanglement-assisted communication, they can enhance its reliability.

</details>


### [136] [LDGM-Based Quantum Codes for Fault-Tolerant Quantum Computation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15159)
*Yumin Li,Kejing Liu,Hanqing Lou,Javier Garcia-Frias*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一类新的CSS码，利用LDGM码的生成矩阵和校验矩阵，通过行操作调整量子速率，采用消息传递迭代译码和离散密度演化优化，在去极化信道中展现出优异的纠错性能，且稳定子生成元权重小，适合容错量子计算。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 构建一类灵活易设计、具有优秀纠错能力且适合容错量子计算的CSS量子码。

Method: 利用LDGM码的生成矩阵和校验矩阵构造CSS码，对矩阵施加行操作以实现所需的量子速率；采用图上的消息传递进行迭代译码，并使用离散密度演化（DDE）优化去极化信道下的性能。

Result: 所构造的量子码在纠错能力方面表现优异，且稳定子生成元的权重可控且较小。

Conclusion: 该构造方法具有高度的灵活性和设计简便性，生成的量子码不仅纠错性能优秀，而且由于稳定子权重低，特别适合容错量子计算应用。

Abstract: We construct a new family of Calderbank-Shor-Steane (CSS) codes using the generator and parity-check matrices of Low-Density Generator Matrix (LDGM) codes, with row operations applied to both matrices in order to achieve the desired quantum rate. Decoding is performed in an iterative manner, by applying message passing over the associated graph, and discrete Density Evolution (DDE) is used to optimize performance in the depolarizing channel. The proposed construction offers high flexibility and easiness in the design, producing quantum codes that possess excellent error correction capabilities. By properly designing the structure of the code, we are able to control and bound the weight of the stabilizer generators to a small value, which results in codes particularly well suited for fault-tolerant quantum computation. At the same time, these codes achieve very good performance in terms of error correction capability.

</details>


### [137] [Parameter-Shift Rules for Gradients in Boson Sampling Experiments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15160)
*Marius Trudeau,Pierre-Emmanuel Emeriau,Nicolás Quesada*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文针对光子量子实验中的损耗模型，推导了Fock玻色子采样跃迁概率梯度的n阶参数偏移规则，并展示了该规则在一般高斯玻色子采样中不可行，仅在特定分解条件下可行。通过实际硬件验证了方法的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在含噪光子量子实验中，玻色子采样跃迁概率的梯度计算是其优化和训练的关键，但现有方法（如有限差分）精度和效率不足，需要更高效的解析梯度计算方法。

Method: 基于鲁棒的光子损耗模型，推导了Fock玻色子采样中n光子通过有损干涉仪时的n阶参数偏移规则；分析了高斯玻色子采样中该规则的推广限制，并给出了传输矩阵可分解为对角损耗矩阵左乘纯酉矩阵时的特例条件。

Result: 成功推导了Fock玻色子采样的n阶参数偏移规则；证明了高斯玻色子采样中一般情况无法推广，仅在特殊分解条件下存在有限阶（2倍总探测光子数）参数偏移规则；在真实硬件上验证了方法优于有限差分。

Conclusion: 本工作为有损光子量子实验中的梯度计算提供了高效解析方法，尤其适用于Fock玻色子采样场景，为量子光学优化和量子机器学习提供了实用工具。

Abstract: Using a robust photon loss model for photonic quantum experiments, we derive $n-$th order parameter-shift rules for computing gradients of Fock boson sampling transition probabilities where $n$ photons are sent into a lossy interferometer. We also show that in general it is not possible to generalize this gradient recipe to the case of Gaussian boson sampling. Only in the specific case where the transmission matrix of the interferometer can be factorized as a diagonal loss matrix premultiplied by a pure unitary it is possible to obtain parameter-shift rules with a finite order given by twice the total number of photons detected. We demonstrate the efficacy of the proposed method by comparing its performance against finite differences on real hardware.

</details>


### [138] [Backpropagating Pauli Propagation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15184)
*Sheng-Hsuan Lin,Etienne Granet,Kevin Hémery,Henrik Dreyer*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种利用泡利传播模拟的量子电路反向传播算法，用于高效计算参数梯度。该方法在计算复杂度上与标准稀疏泡利模拟相当，梯度精度与可观测量期望值同阶。通过利用量子电路的可逆性，内存开销比传统反向模式自动微分降低O(n_param)倍；相比有限差分法，函数评估效率提升O(n_param)倍。在横向伊辛模型和海森堡模型的低能态制备以及二维时间演化电路压缩中进行了验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的量子电路参数梯度计算方法存在效率瓶颈：反向模式自动微分内存成本高，有限差分法需要大量函数评估。需要一种既高效又精确的梯度计算方法来支持量子电路的经典优化。

Method: 提出基于泡利传播模拟的反向传播算法，利用量子电路的可逆性降低内存开销，通过泡利轨迹模拟实现梯度计算，在计算复杂度上与标准稀疏泡利模拟技术相当。

Result: 梯度精度与可观测量期望值同阶；内存成本比传统反向模式自动微分降低O(n_param)倍；函数评估效率比有限差分法提升O(n_param)倍。在一维、二维、三维横向伊辛模型和三维海森堡模型的低能态制备，以及二维时间演化电路压缩中验证了有效性。

Conclusion: 该方法实现了量子电路参数梯度的高效精确计算，支持在优化过程中监测和正则化算子复杂性度量（如算子稳定子Rényi熵），适用于态制备和时间演化压缩等量子电路经典优化任务。

Abstract: We develop a backpropagation algorithm for evaluating parameter gradients in quantum circuits using Pauli propagation simulation. The method has computational complexity comparable to that of standard sparse Pauli simulation techniques, while producing gradients whose accuracy is of the same order as the corresponding observable expectation values. By exploiting the reversibility of quantum circuits, the algorithm reduces the memory cost by a factor of $\mathcal{O}(n_\text{param})$ compared with conventional reverse-mode automatic differentiation, where $n_\text{param}$ denotes the number of parameters in the circuit. Compared with finite difference methods, the algorithm is $\mathcal{O}(n_\text{param})$ more efficient in function evaluations. These features enable efficient and accurate classical optimization of quantum circuits for applications such as state preparation and time-evolution compression, while also allowing operator-complexity measures such as the operator stabilizer Rényi entropy to be monitored and regularized during optimization. We demonstrate the method by optimizing low-energy state-preparation circuits for transverse-field Ising models in one, two, and three dimensions and for the three-dimensional Heisenberg model, and by compressing two-dimensional time-evolution circuits.

</details>


### [139] [Entanglement Detection for Two-Qubit and Three-Qubit Pure States via Unitary Transformations and Ancilla State Measurements](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15201)
*Yu-Hang Liu,Yuan-Hong Tao,Yi-Wen Liu,Ying-Huan Zhou,Shao-Ming Fei*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于酉变换和辅助测量的量子电路方案，可直接测量两体并发度和三体3-tangle纠缠度量，无需进行完整量子态层析，为纠缠特性的实验表征提供了可行技术路径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 纠缠是量子力学的核心特征和量子信息处理的关键资源，但现有纠缠检测与量化方法在高效直接测量方面仍存在不足，需要发展无需完整量子态层析的直接测量方案。

Method: 通过引入辅助量子比特并构建特定的受控酉操作，将两体并发度和三体3-tangle纠缠度量的解析表达式映射到量子电路输出态的测量概率上。

Result: 提出了一组能够直接测量两体并发度和三体3-tangle的量子电路方案，实现了对两体和三体纠缠的高效直接定量测量。

Conclusion: 该工作为纠缠特性的实验表征提供了可行技术路线，为多体纠缠资源在量子信息处理中的实际部署奠定了基础。

Abstract: Quantum entanglement is the fundamental hallmark of quantum mechanics and a core resource for realizing long-distance quantum communication and scalable linear quantum computing. Accordingly, the precise detection and quantitative quantification of entanglement constitute a foundational and critical problem in quantum information theory. To date, researchers have proposed numerous sufficient conditions for entanglement detection as well as a variety of entanglement measures to characterize the entanglement strength of quantum states; nevertheless, efficient and direct measurement schemes for core entanglement parameters remain underdeveloped. Based on unitary transformations and auxiliary measurements, this paper proposes a set of quantum circuit schemes capable of directly measuring the bipartite concurrence and the tripartite 3-tangle entanglement measure. By introducing auxiliary qubits and constructing specific controlled unitary operations, the proposed scheme maps the analytical expressions of the two entanglement measures onto the measurement probabilities of output states from quantum circuits. It enables efficient and direct quantitative measurement of bipartite and tripartite entanglement without performing full quantum state tomography. This work provides a feasible technical route for the experimental characterization of entanglement properties and lays a groundwork for the practical deployment of multipartite entanglement resources in quantum information processing.

</details>


### [140] [Counterexamples to additivity of minimum output $p$-Rényi entropy of quantum channels for $p>3/4$ and $0\leq p<1/4$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15210)
*Debbie Leung,Benjamin Lovitz,Peixue Wu*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文证明了对于所有Rényi阶p>3/4或0≤p<1/4，存在有限维投影诱导量子通道使得最小输出p-Rényi熵的可加性失效，将未解决区间缩小至[1/4,3/4]。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 最小输出熵的可加性是量子信息论的核心问题，已知在p>1、p=1及p接近0时存在非可加性，但0<p<1的大部分区间仍未解决。

Method: 结合两种相关随机投影构造：对于p>3/4使用乘积共轭Bell态见证，对于p<1/4使用转置补秩缺陷见证。

Result: 证明了在p>3/4或0≤p<1/4时，存在有限维投影诱导量子通道使得最小输出p-Rényi熵的可加性失效，并将未解决区间缩小至[1/4,3/4]。

Conclusion: 改进了最小输出von Neumann熵可加性违背的输出维度阈值，将开放问题范围大幅缩减。

Abstract: Additivity of minimum output entropies is a central problem in quantum information theory. Nonadditivity is known for every Rényi order $p>1$, at the von Neumann point $p=1$, and near $p=0$, while most of the interval $0<p<1$ has remained open. We prove that for every Rényi order $p$ satisfying either $p>3/4$ or $0\leq p<1/4$, there exist finite-dimensional projection-induced quantum channels such that additivity of the minimum output $p$-Rényi entropy fails. The proof combines two correlated random-projection constructions: a product-conjugate Bell-state witness for $p>3/4$, and a transpose-complement rank-defect witness for $p<1/4$. Thus the unresolved part of $0<p<1$ is reduced to $[1/4,3/4]$. Our estimates also improve the output dimension threshold for additivity violation of minimum output von Neumann entropy, first established in Belinschi, Collins and Nechida.

</details>


### [141] [Coulomb blockade in microscopic material defects as a source of decoherence and noise in solid-state quantum circuits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15252)
*R. Banerjee,L. P. Lindoy,M. Hegedus,A. Hutcheson,T. Hawkins,E. Daghigh-Ahmadi,S. Samaddar,T. Barker,J. P. Goff,A. Ya. Tzalenchuk,I. Rungger,S. E. de Graaf*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文通过扫描栅极显微镜在超导电路中识别出一种由金属颗粒中的库仑阻塞和微波驱动电荷隧穿引起的新的退相干机制，这种机制与传统的两能级系统缺陷同样普遍且有害，挑战了当前认为相干寿命主要受限于TLS缺陷的主流范式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 固态量子器件的退相干机制与其微观材料起源之间的关联长期以来难以建立，阻碍了相干性的提升。现有研究未能充分识别源自金属颗粒的退相干机制。

Method: 使用扫描栅极显微镜对工作中的超导电路进行实时成像，表征多个不同器件中的缺陷，并分析其物理机制。

Result: 发现金属颗粒中的库仑阻塞和微波驱动电荷隧穿是一种新的普遍退相干源，其危害程度与TLS缺陷相当，但物理机制根本不同；传统表征技术会将其误归因于其他过程。

Conclusion: 消除制造过程中的金属颗粒可有效抑制该退相干机制，为改善超导量子器件的相干性和降低噪声提供了明确的实用路径。

Abstract: A critical limitation of solid-state quantum devices arises from the materials from which they are fabricated: uncontrolled surfaces, interfaces, and structural imperfections introduce numerous sources of loss and decoherence. Despite extensive efforts, linking these decoherence mechanisms to their microscopic material origins -- essential for developing effective mitigation strategies -- remains an outstanding challenge that has slowed coherence improvements. Here, using scanning gate microscopy on live superconducting circuits we identify a previously unrecognised decoherence mechanism originating from Coulomb blockade and microwave-driven charge tunnelling in metallic grains. Such grains are ubiquitous in thin-film devices fabricated by standard lithography processes. By characterising multiple defects across different devices, we find such defects to be as common and as debilitating to device performance as two-level system (TLS) defects, while originating from a fundamentally different physical mechanism. Importantly, conventional characterisation techniques would misattribute this loss to other, microwave power-independent processes. Our observations thus reveal a widespread source of decoherence in superconducting circuits, challenging the prevailing paradigm that coherence lifetimes are primarily limited by TLS defects. Eliminating metallic grains during fabrication provides a clear and practical route to suppress this mechanism, offering a pathway towards improved coherence and reduced noise in microwave-based solid-state quantum devices.

</details>


### [142] [Dynamic Entanglement Distribution for Multi-User and Multi-Protocol Quantum Networking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15262)
*Rui Wang,Marcus J. Clark,Obada Alia,Sima Bahrani,Djeylan Aktas,Matej Peranić,Mario Stipčević,Martin Lončarić,John Rarity,Siddarth K. Joshi,Dimitra Simeonidou*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文展示了一种基于量子可重构光分插复用器(q-ROADM)的城域纠缠量子通信网络，能够动态地将偏振纠缠光子对从宽带源分发至六个用户，支持全网格、部分网格和切片子网络等多种可编程配置，并实现了超过150小时的稳定运行，同时支持多种量子协议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可扩展、多用户、多协议的量子网络需要动态的纠缠分发能力，现有方案在灵活性和可重构性方面存在不足。

Method: 采用量子可重构光分插复用器(q-ROADM)架构，在已部署的校园和城域光纤上动态分发宽带源产生的偏振纠缠光子对，支持全网格、部分网格和切片子网络等可编程拓扑配置。

Result: 实现了六用户超过150小时的稳定全网格运行；比较了全网格与时分部分网格策略在不同源和探测器条件下的性能；实现了量子网络切片与可选互联链路；在同一基础设施上展示了SIAT协议与网络泛洪结合的多路径安全新用户接入。

Conclusion: q-ROADM使能的纠缠分发架构为可重构、面向服务的量子光网络提供了新途径。

Abstract: Dynamic entanglement distribution is a key requirement for scalable, multi-user and multi-protocol quantum networks. We demonstrate a metropolitan-scale entanglement-based quantum communication network enabled by a quantum reconfigurable optical add-drop multiplexer (q-ROADM), which dynamically distributes polarisation-entangled photon pairs from a broadband source to six users over deployed campus and metropolitan fibre. The network supports programmable full-mesh, partial-mesh and sliced sub-network configurations, enabling flexible allocation of entanglement resources according to link condition and service requirement. We demonstrate stable six-user full-mesh operation over more than 150 hours, compare full-mesh and time-shared partial-mesh strategies under different source and detector conditions, and realise quantum network slicing with optional/additional interconnection links. We also show that the same infrastructure can support different quantum protocols by showcasing Secure Inaugural Authentication-Transfer (SIAT) combined with Network flooding over multiple paths to improve the security of onboarding a new user. These results demonstrate a q-ROADM-enabled entanglement distribution architecture as a novel route towards reconfigurable, service-oriented quantum networking over optical fibre infrastructure.

</details>


### [143] [Locality of deep thermalisation through the lens of entanglement teleportation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15276)
*Saptarshi Mandal,Alan Sherry,Sthitadhi Roy*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了由于对互补子系统进行投影测量而导致的子系统上通用量子态系综的出现现象——深度热化。当子系统被划分为两个始终因果断开的子区域时，深度热化的发生受到测量诱导的纠缠传送的根本限制。在一般局域相互作用系统中，深度热化和纠缠传送的时间尺度随子区域间距离呈对数增长，表现出涌现的局域性；而在某些特殊电路中，测量结果的随机性被完美传递，深度热化的时间尺度有限，导致真正的非局域性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究深度热化现象中的局域性（或非局域性）问题，特别是当子系统被划分为两个始终因果断开的子区域时，深度热化如何发生及其时间尺度。

Method: 将子系统划分为两个因果断开的子区域，研究测量诱导的纠缠传送与深度热化之间的关系，分析一般局域相互作用系统与特殊电路两种情形下的时间尺度缩放行为。

Result: 一般局域相互作用系统中，深度热化和纠缠传送的时间尺度随子区域间距离呈对数增长；特殊电路中，测量结果随机性被完美传递至子系统态系综，深度热化时间尺度有限。

Conclusion: 深度热化在因果断开几何中受测量诱导纠缠传送限制，一般系统表现出涌现的局域性，但存在特殊电路可实现真正的非局域性。

Abstract: Deep thermalisation characterises the emergence of universal quantum state ensembles on subsystems due to projective measurements on their complement. We study the notion of locality, or lack thereof, in this phenomenon by considering a subsystem partitioned into two disjoint subregions which remain causally disconnected at all times under unitary dynamics. We show that the onset of deep thermalisation in this geometry is fundamentally bounded by measurement-induced entanglement teleportation between the subregions. While measurements on the environment generate entanglement across the disconnected partitions -- suggesting an apparent non-locality -- we demonstrate that generic locally interacting systems exhibit an emergent locality. Specifically, the timescales for both deep thermalisation and entanglement teleportation scale logarithmically with the distance separating the subregions. Exceptions to this include special circuits where the randomness of the measurement outcomes is perfectly transmitted to the ensemble of states of the subsystem, conditioned on the outcomes; in such cases the timescale for deep thermalisation is finite leading to genuine non-locality.

</details>

### [144] [Thin-Shell Wormholes from Entropy-Induced Black-Hole Geometries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14130)
*Jonathan A. Rebouças,Edson Otoniel*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文从修正黑洞熵出发，推导了薄壳虫洞的Darmois-Israel匹配条件，研究了多种熵模型（Bekenstein-Hawking、Barrow、Tsallis-Cirto、Rényi、Kaniadakis、对数、圈量子引力启发和指数熵）下的对称切割-粘贴虫洞。结果表明，所有正lapse域中对称构造需要负表面能量密度；恒定barotropic分支均线性不稳定，而变参数Chaplygin壳层可支持稳定构型。稳定性由熵几何、喉径和壳层状态方程径向指数共同决定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 建立基于修正黑洞熵的薄壳虫洞统一热力学框架，探究不同熵变形对虫洞几何结构、表面应力和稳定性的影响。

Method: 从熵诱导的lapse函数$F_{\mathcal S}(r)=1-4πM/\mathcal S'(r)$出发，构造对称切割-粘贴虫洞，将Darmois-Israel匹配条件直接用lapse和熵导数表达，推导表面应力、能量条件组合、守恒方程和径向有效势，并应用于八种熵模型。

Result: 对称构造在所有正lapse域中需要负表面能量密度；恒定barotropic分支均线性不稳定；变参数Chaplygin壳层可支持稳定构型，稳定性由熵几何、喉径和状态方程径向指数共同决定。

Conclusion: 建立了比较熵诱导黑洞几何作为薄壳虫洞种子的统一框架，发现稳定性不单独由熵变形决定，而是取决于熵几何与喉部局域物质动力学响应之间的相互作用。

Abstract: Modified black-hole entropies can induce effective spacetime geometries and thereby provide a thermodynamic route for investigating thin-shell wormholes. In this work, we construct symmetric cut-and-paste wormholes from the generic entropic lapse function $F_{\mathcal S}(r)=1-4πM/\mathcal S'(r)$ and formulate the Darmois--Israel junction conditions directly in terms of the lapse and of the entropy derivatives. We derive the surface stresses, shell energy-condition combinations, conservation equation, and radial effective potential, and then apply the formalism to the Bekenstein--Hawking, Barrow, Tsallis--Cirto, Rényi, Kaniadakis, logarithmic, loop-quantum-gravity-inspired, and exponential entropy prescriptions. The analysis shows that the symmetric construction requires negative surface energy density throughout every admissible positive-lapse domain, although entropy deformations can significantly modify the horizon structure, the allowed throat region, and the localization of the surface stresses. Within the parameter domains considered here, all examined constant-barotropic branches are linearly radially unstable, despite quantitative changes in their near-horizon scales. In contrast, a variable Chaplygin shell can support stable configurations, with the stability domains determined jointly by the entropic geometry, the throat radius, and the radial exponent of the shell equation of state. These results establish a unified framework for comparing entropy-induced black-hole geometries as thin-shell wormhole seeds and show that stability is governed not by the entropy deformation alone, but by its interplay with the dynamical response of the matter localized at the throat.

</details>


### [145] [A note on the conformal geometry of the 2-sphere](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14133)
*Jörg Frauendiener*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文讨论二维球面的几何，重点研究将给定度规通过共形因子缩放为圆球度规的问题。发现存在唯一的对称无迹张量补充Ricci张量构成无散对称张量，由此推导出一个线性二阶偏微分方程，其解空间具有自然Minkowski结构。证明存在无穷多个这样的空间，它们通过可视为由"4-向量"参数化的等距变换相联系。最后证明每个二维球面都能以典范方式等距嵌入到Minkowski空间中。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究二维球面上共形因子将给定度规变为圆球度规的几何结构，并寻找其与Minkowski空间之间的内在联系。

Method: 通过构造唯一的对称无迹张量来补充Ricci张量，得到无散对称张量；利用该张量推导线性二阶偏微分方程；分析解空间的Minkowski结构及其等距变换参数化；证明二维球面到Minkowski空间的典范等距嵌入。

Result: 找到了一个对称无迹张量，导出了具有Minkowski结构的线性二阶PDE；发现了无穷多个由"4-向量"参数化的等距变换空间；证明了每个2-球面都能典范地等距嵌入Minkowski空间。

Conclusion: 二维球面的共形几何与Minkowski结构之间存在深刻的内在联系，球面可以自然地嵌入Minkowski空间，且这种嵌入具有典范性。

Abstract: We discuss the geometry of the two-dimensional sphere. Our perspective focuses on the set of conformal factors that rescale a given metric to a round metric. We find that there exists a unique symmetric and trace-free tensor on the sphere which complements the Ricci tensor to a divergence-free symmetric tensor. Using this tensor, we derive a linear second-order PDE whose solution space carries a natural Minkowski structure. We show that there are in fact infinitely many such spaces, all related by isometries which can be thought of as being parametrised by ``4-vectors''. Finally, we show that every 2-sphere can be isometrically embedded in a Minkowski space in a canonical way.

</details>


### [146] [An effective Friedmann equation for a bouncing anisotropic universe](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14153)
*Syed Moeez Hassan*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文推导了反弹聚合物量子化Bianchi-I时空的广义有效弗里德曼和瑞乔杜里方程，并证明了反弹时物质能量密度与各向异性剪切之间的关系对任何物质类型都精确成立，同时给出了该关系中常数的显式表达式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前文献中关于反弹时物质能量密度与各向异性剪切之间的关系是基于数值推导的，缺乏理论上的严格证明。本文旨在从理论上精确推导该关系，并给出其常数的显式表达式，以完善聚合物量子化Bianchi-I宇宙学的理论基础。

Method: 通过推导广义有效弗里德曼方程和瑞乔杜里方程，对聚合物量子化的Bianchi-I时空进行分析，从理论上证明物质能量密度与各向异性剪切在反弹点的精确关系。

Result: 成功推导了广义有效场方程；证明了反弹时能量密度与各向异性剪切的关系对任意物质内容均精确成立；给出了该关系中常数的显式解析表达式。

Conclusion: 该工作为聚合物量子化Bianchi-I宇宙学提供了更坚实的理论基础，使得原先仅通过数值手段得到的关系获得了严格的解析证明，有助于进一步理解量子宇宙反弹的动力学行为。

Abstract: We derive generalized effective Friedmann and Raychaudhuri equations for a bouncing polymer quantized Bianchi-I spacetime. We further prove that the relation numerically derived in the literature between the matter energy density and the anisotropic shear at the bounce holds exactly for any type of matter content, and derive explicit expressions for the constants that appear in that relation.

</details>


### [147] [Reconstructions of Horndeski Subclasses by Gaussian Processes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14154)
*Marco C. Mendoza Marcos,Ana A. Avilez Lopez*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文利用宇宙学观测数据（宇宙时钟、重子声振荡）通过高斯过程贝叶斯技术重建了Horndeski理论的两个子类——Brans-Dicke和Cubic Galileon理论。研究发现Brans-Dicke子类在红移0.4-1.7区间优于ΛCDM模型，而Cubic Galileon子类在全部观测红移范围内都倾向于标量场与引力耦合且暗能量密度随时间演化的宇宙学。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在Horndeski理论框架下，利用最新的宇宙学观测数据重建两个子类理论，检验修正引力理论是否比标准ΛCDM模型更好地描述宇宙晚期演化。

Method: 采用高斯过程贝叶斯技术，利用宇宙时钟（Cosmic Chronometers）的哈勃率模型无关测量以及重子声振荡（BAO）的哈勃率和距离测量数据，对Brans-Dicke和Cubic Galileon两个Horndeski子类理论进行重建。

Result: Brans-Dicke子类在红移0.4-1.7范围内比ΛCDM模型更受数据青睐；Cubic Galileon子类在全部观测红移范围内都显示出标量场与引力耦合且暗能量密度随时间演化的特征。

Conclusion: 观测数据支持修正引力理论中引力常数在宇宙学尺度上的时间变化，且两种子类理论都能得到与数据一致的重建结果，其中Cubic Galileon子类特别倾向于随时间演化的暗能量密度。

Abstract: In this work we reconstruct two subclasses of theories from the Horndeski class, the most general scalar-tensor theories of gravity with second-order equations, according to a variety of late-universe cosmological data: model independent measurements of Hubble rate from Cosmic Chronometers, as well as Hubble rate and distance measurements from Baryonic Acoustic Oscillations. In the modified gravity family considered, the scalar field is non-minimally coupled to gravity leading to time variations in the gravitational constant at cosmological scales. The theories considered are also consistent with constraints on the propagation speed of gravitational waves. The reconstruction is carried out by using the Gaussian Processes bayesian technique either for the Brans-Dicke and the Cubic Galileon subclasses of Horndeski theory. We find that the most likely Gaussian Process reconstruction for the data favors a cosmology from the Brans-Dicke subclass over $Λ$CDM in the range $z \in (0.4, 1.7)$, and for the Cubic Galileon subclass we find that the most likely reconstruction indeed favors modifications of the scalar field to gravity with time evolving dark energy density in the whole range of redshift observations.

</details>


### [148] [Breaking the Degeneracy: Spectral Hardening of Accretion Disks around Rotating Hayward Black Holes in Dark Matter Halos](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14164)
*Sandip Dutta*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了在宏观暗物质包层中旋转的规则Hayward黑洞周围的薄吸积盘的热力学和可观测特征，发现量子修正核心与暗物质晕对辐射效率有叠加增强作用（自旋j=0.8时可达~14.4%），并揭示了纯几何（Hayward真空）与纯天体物理（Kerr+DM）构型在低频到中频区存在宏观简并性，该简并仅在极端Wien尾波段被打破，为非奇异黑洞与经典暗物质环境的区分提供了关键诊断手段。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索旋转规则Hayward黑洞在暗物质包层中的吸积盘观测特征，并区分纯几何非奇异性与宏观暗物质环境对可观测信号的影响。

Method: 构建三区域复合时空模型（内层Hayward核心+中间指数密度暗物质壳层+外渐近平直真空），通过质量分布匹配区域，计算ISCO、辐射效率、热通量及多色黑体谱光度，并在四种构型（Kerr真空、Hayward真空、Kerr+DM、Hayward+DM）间进行系统比较。

Result: 量子核心与暗物质晕对ISCO有严格层级压缩效应，对辐射效率有叠加增强作用（自旋j=0.8时~14.4%）；Hayward真空与Kerr+DM构型在低频至中频区存在宏观简并性，该简并仅在极端Wien尾波段被打破。

Conclusion: 非奇异黑洞与暗物质环境产生的观测信号在多数波段不可区分，只有高频率谱偏振观测才能打破简并，为未来高频率谱偏振测量区分两类模型提供了关键诊断依据。

Abstract: We investigate the thermodynamic and observable signatures of thin accretion disks surrounding rotating, regular Hayward black holes embedded within a macroscopic dark matter (DM) envelope. The spacetime is rigorously modelled as a three-region composite: an inner Hayward core regularised by a de~Sitter limit, an intermediate DM shell modelled as a pressureless dust envelope governed by an exponential sphere density profile, and an asymptotically flat outer vacuum. By matching these regions via the mass profile, we compute the explicit modifications to the Innermost Stable Circular Orbit (ISCO), radiative efficiency, local thermal flux, and multi-colour blackbody spectral luminosity. A systematic comparison across four distinct configurations (Kerr vacuum, Hayward vacuum, Kerr\,+\,DM, and Hayward\,+\,DM) reveals a strict hierarchical compression of the ISCO. We demonstrate that the quantum-inspired core regularity and the macroscopic DM halo exert an additive enhancement on the radiative efficiency, reaching $\sim 14.4\%$ for highly spinning black holes ($j=0.8$). However, this additive behaviour introduces a profound macroscopic degeneracy between purely geometric (Hayward vacuum) and purely astrophysical (Kerr\,+\,DM) configurations at low-to-intermediate frequencies. We establish that this degeneracy is structurally broken only in the extreme Wien tail of the multi-wavelength spectrum, providing a critical diagnostic footprint for future high-frequency spectropolarimetry to distinguish non-singular black holes from classical dark matter environments.

</details>


### [149] [Probing Quantum Gravity through Chaotic Orbits and Strong-Field Effects in Kerr Black Holes Embedded in Perfect Fluid Dark Matter](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14170)
*Shubham Kala,Sara Saghafi,M. Yousaf,Hemwati Nandan,Ahmadjon Abdujabbarov,Chengxun Yuan,G. Mustafa*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 该论文利用庞加莱截面、李雅普诺夫指数、KS熵和加权伯克霍夫平均等方法，研究了量子改进旋转黑洞在完美流体暗物质环境中的非线性光子动力学，揭示了量子改进参数和暗物质参数对光子运动从规则到混沌转变的显著影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究量子引力修正和完美流体暗物质（PFDM）共同作用下旋转黑洞周围光子动力学的非线性行为，特别是混沌动力学特征及其对黑洞阴影和强场引力透镜的潜在观测影响。

Method: 采用庞加莱截面、李雅普诺夫指数、Kolmogorov-Sinai (KS)熵和加权伯克霍夫平均（WBA）等多种分析方法，研究量子改进参数和PFDM参数对零测地线运动及圆形轨道稳定性的影响。

Result: 随着量子改进参数和PFDM参数增加，庞加莱截面展示从规则运动到混沌运动的转变，不变环面变形碎裂；李雅普诺夫指标表明光子轨迹对初始条件的敏感性增强；KS熵证实混沌行为增长；WBA方法提供了区分规则与混沌轨道的定量判据。

Conclusion: 量子引力修正和暗物质贡献显著改变了光子运动的有效势，导致规则与混沌区域共存的丰富混合相空间结构，对黑洞阴影和强场引力透镜具有重要的观测意义。

Abstract: We study the nonlinear photon dynamics in quantum improved rotating black hole surrounded by perfect fluid dark matter (PFDM) using several methods of analysis, including Poincaré sections, Lyapunov exponents, Kolmogorov-Sinai (KS) entropy and weighted Birkhoff averages (WBA). In particular, we examine the effect of quantum-improved parameter $(\tildeω)$ and PFDM parameter $(ζ)$ on the null geodesic motion hence stability of circular orbit. Poincaré sections illustrate the transition from regular to chaotic motion as these parameters increase, characterized by the deformation and fragmentation of invariant tori and the emergence of scattered chaotic regions in phase space. The stability properties of null circular orbits are quantified through the Lyapunov indicators, revealing that both the quantum improvement parameter and the PFDM parameter enhance the sensitivity of photon trajectories to initial conditions. The KS entropy provides an independent measure of dynamical complexity and confirms the growth of chaotic behavior with increasing quantum and PFDM corrections. Additionally, the WBA method offers a robust quantitative criterion for distinguishing regular and chaotic orbits and allows a detailed mapping of the phase-space structure. The results demonstrate that the combined effects of quantum gravity corrections and PFDM significantly modify the effective potential governing photon motion, leading to a rich mixed phase-space structure with coexisting regular and chaotic regions. These findings underscore the crucial role of quantum and dark matter contributions in shaping photon dynamics near rotating black holes and suggest possible observational implications on black hole shadows and gravitational lensing in strong-field regimes.

</details>


### [150] [Novel topological subclass in Bardeen-AdS-class black holes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14173)
*Yu-Die Wan,Peng Zhao,Zheng-Wen Long*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文基于φ-映射拓扑流理论，系统研究了规则Bardeen-AdS类黑洞的热力学拓扑，在W⁰⁻拓扑族中发现了一个新的内部子类$\widetilde{W}^{0-}$，该子类具有全局拓扑电荷W=0和内-外视界缠绕数符号$[-,+]$，并通过两个临界耦合参数划分了不同的拓扑相。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 规则Bardeen-AdS类黑洞（非线性电动力学耦合的无奇点几何）的拓扑分类尚未被充分探索，现有五拓扑类四拓扑子类的框架中，W⁰⁻族的内部支结构有待细化和丰富。

Method: 基于φ-映射拓扑流理论，在AdS半径L=1和L=15两个典型值下进行数值计算，通过分析缠绕数符号、全局拓扑电荷及参数空间中的临界耦合参数来识别新的拓扑子类。

Result: 在W⁰⁻拓扑族中识别出新的内部子类$\widetilde{W}^{0-}$（全局拓扑电荷W=0，内-外视界缠绕数$[-,+]$）；确定了两个临界参数$\hat{m}_{01}$和$\hat{m}_{02}$，将参数空间划分为Type I（$m_0\ge\hat{m}_{01}$）、Type II（$\hat{m}_{02}<m_0<\hat{m}_{01}$）属于$\widetilde{W}^{0-}$，以及$0<m_0\le\hat{m}_{02}$属于传统W⁰⁻拓扑。

Conclusion: 验证了热力学拓扑形式主义对无奇点规则黑洞的自洽性，提炼了针对非线性电动力学规则黑洞的拓扑判别标准，为后续高维旋转规则黑洞的拓扑研究提供了理论参考。

Abstract: Based on the $φ$-mapping topological current theory, we systematically investigate the thermodynamic topology of regular Bardeen-AdS-class black holes coupled to nonlinear electrodynamics, a class of singularity-free geometries whose topological classification remains underexplored. We implement numerical calculations at two typical AdS radii $L=1$ and $L=15$. Within the fundamental $W^{0-}$ topological family, we identify a novel inner secondary subclass $\widetilde{W}^{0-}$. This result only refines and enriches the internal branch structure of the $W^{0-}$ family under the established classification scheme of five topological classes and four topological subclasses for black hole thermodynamics. This new subclass has a vanishing global topological charge $W=0$ with an inner-outer horizon winding-number signature $[-,+]$. Unlike standard $W^{0-}$ solutions that realize topological extension via embedded $(+,-)$ winding pairs, $\widetilde{W}^{0-}$ arises from attaching an extra stable branch to the end of the base solution sequence. Two critical coupling parameters $\hat{m}_{01}$ and $\hat{m}_{02}$ divide the full parameter space into distinct topological phases: Type I black holes with $m_0\ge\hat{m}_{01}$ and Type II solutions with $\hat{m}_{02}<m_0<\hat{m}_{01}$ belong to $\widetilde{W}^{0-}$, while the parameter region $0<m_0\le\hat{m}_{02}$ corresponds to the conventional $W^{0-}$ topology. Our numerical results verify the self-consistency of the thermodynamic topological formalism when applied to singularity-free regular black holes, refine the topological discrimination criteria tailored to nonlinear-electrodynamics regular black holes, and provide theoretical references for subsequent topological investigations of higher-dimensional rotating regular black holes under the same formalism.

</details>


### [151] [Coupled by Design: Computing Kerr-Newman Quasinormal Modes with a Hybrid SpectralPINN Solver](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14216)
*Alexandre M. Pombo*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文扩展了\texttt{SpectralPINN}求解器，用于计算Kerr-Newman黑洞的准正规模式，求解两个耦合的主偏微分方程组，从可分离的未荷电Kerr极限推进到真正的两场耦合问题。研究发现了两种解族：光子球族（与Kerr连接）和近视界族（与Kerr极限断开），各有两个分支（引力主导和矢量主导）。首次系统计算并表征了矢量主导光子球分支。验证显示频率相对误差最差约$10^{-4}$，多数情况约$10^{-7}$。数据集应用于观测特征值排斥现象、排除域内极化间排斥，并预测Einstein望远镜对黑洞电荷质量比的约束。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有Kerr-Newman黑洞准正规模式的计算主要基于可分离的未荷电Kerr极限，但荷电情况下引力场与电磁场耦合导致方程组无法分离，缺乏针对耦合两场问题的系统求解方法和完整的数据集，尤其是矢量主导光子球分支从未被系统表征过。

Method: 将\texttt{SpectralPINN}求解器从单方程扩展到求解两个耦合主偏微分方程组，采用谱方法与物理信息神经网络相结合的方式数值求解。计算了五种光子球模式（引力主导和矢量主导，最高$\ell=4$）以及两种基态引力主导近视界模式。

Result: 与公开数据集基准对比，频率相对误差最差情况约$10^{-4}$，多数情况达$10^{-7}$。首次系统计算并表征了矢量主导光子球分支。应用数据集观测到特征值排斥现象，排除了域内极化间排斥，并预测了Einstein望远镜对黑洞电荷质量比的约束能力。

Conclusion: 本文成功将谱PINN方法扩展到处理耦合两场问题，提供了首个系统的Kerr-Newman准正规模式数据集（含矢量主导分支），并展示了该数据集在天体物理观测（Einstein望远镜）约束黑洞电荷方面的应用价值。

Abstract: We extend our \texttt{SpectralPINN} solver to the computation of Kerr-Newman quasinormal modes by applying it to solve the system of two coupled master PDEs -- advancing from the single, separable equation of the uncharged Kerr limit to a genuinely coupled two-field problem. The coupling between the gravitational and electromagnetic fields gives rise to two families of solutions: the photon-sphere, connecting with Kerr; and near-horizon, disconnected from the Kerr limit, each with two branches of solutions depending on the leading field: the gravitational- and vector-led. Benchmarking against publicly available datasets shows relative frequency errors of $\sim 10^{-4}$ worst case and $\sim10^{-7}$ for most cases. The computed public dataset spans five photon-sphere modes, both gravitational- and vector-led, up to $\ell=4$, as well as two fundamental gravitational-led near-horizon modes. The vector-led photon-sphere branch is computed and systematically characterized for the first time. We apply the dataset and observe the onset of the eigenvalue repulsion reported by Dias \textit{et al.}, to exclude an inter-polarization repulsion within the resolved domain, and to forecast Einstein Telescope constraints on the black-hole charge-to-mass ratio.

</details>


### [152] [Acceleration radiation and HBAR thermodynamics for atoms falling into a BTZ black hole: A CQM quantum-optics approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14281)
*H. E. Camblong,A. Chakraborty,C. R. Ordóñez,M. O. Scully,G. Valdivia-Mera,H. Wang*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 研究了落入BTZ黑洞的原子在Boulware真空中辐射出具有Hawking温度普朗克谱的热辐射，并发现辐射场的光子熵HBAR模仿了Bekenstein-Hawking熵S_BH=A/4，展示了近视界共形量子力学对黑洞热力学性质的普适控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究落入BTZ黑洞的自由原子是否会产生Hawking-like辐射，以及低维（2+1）引力背景下黑洞的热力学和辐射性质是否仍由近视界共形量子力学普适描述。

Method: 采用量子光学方法，将原子视为探测器，分析原子在Boulware-like真空中自由落入BTZ黑洞时与场的相互作用，利用近视界共形量子力学框架计算辐射谱和熵。

Result: 发现原子辐射出Hawking温度下的Planck谱；辐射场的光子熵HBAR精确等于Bekenstein-Hawking熵S_BH=A/4，比例因子为1/4。

Conclusion: 尽管（2+1）维引力具有拓扑性质，BTZ黑洞的热力学和辐射性质仍由近视界共形量子力学普适控制，且该共形行为适用于任意维度黑洞背景下的所有场。

Abstract: Atoms falling freely into a Bañados-Teitelboim-Zanelli (BTZ) black hole in a Boulware-like vacuum are shown to emit radiation with a Planck spectrum at the Hawking temperature $T_{H}$. This leads to thermal Hawking-like radiation for a cloud of falling atoms prepared with random initial times. Moreover, the radiation is related to the relative equivalence principle, with the vacuum field modes accelerated with respect to the falling atom. The physics of the atom-field interactions is most easily described within a quantum optics approach, where each atom can be interpreted as a detector. Despite the topological nature of gravity in $(2+1)$ dimensions, the thermodynamic and radiation properties of BTZ black holes are still universally governed by the same near-horizon conformal quantum mechanics (CQM) applicable to higher-dimensional gravity. This universal conformal behavior is exhibited by all fields in the background of generic black holes, and generates an HBAR entropy $S_{\mathcal P}$ associated with the photon radiation field that mimics the Bekenstein-Hawking entropy $S_{\mathrm{BH}}=A/4$, proportional to the black-hole horizon area, and with the correct $1/4$ proportionality factor.

</details>


### [153] [Upwind embedded boundary SBP operators: New high order numerical schemes for arbitrarily shaped domains with Cartesian grids](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14403)
*Conner Dailey*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文基于嵌入式边界求和分部（SBP）方法，推导了高阶精度且具有更好色散关系保持特性的新型迎风格式算子，并通过三维多块网格上的曲标量波动方程数值实验验证了其鲁棒性和准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 提升嵌入式边界SBP方法的内部和边界闭合格子精度，并改善算子的谱特性，以更好地处理如黑洞切除模拟等非线性问题中的波动方程。

Method: 在嵌入式边界框架下推导新型算子，通过引入匹配边界闭包精度的插值/外推算子，推广至迎风格式以优化色散关系保持特性，并最小化边界误差。

Result: 新算子提高了内部和边界闭合阶数，改善了谱特性；在包含切除球面的三维多块网格上，使用曲标量波动方程测试验证了鲁棒性和准确性。

Conclusion: 新型嵌入式边界SBP算子能够在保持高阶精度的同时改善波动问题的色散特性，适用于复杂边界条件下的非线性数值模拟。

Abstract: Embedded boundary summation by parts (SBP) methods define finite differencing based derivative operators with the added feature that the boundary need not coincide with a grid cell, allowing a boundary to be embedded on a regular Cartesian grid. This is achieved by the introduction of interpolation/extrapolation operators that match the accuracy of the boundary closure. These methods have been used to perform black hole excision simulations on a domain with a spherical boundary embedded in a regular Cartesian grid, demonstrating their usefulness for nonlinear problems. In this work, new operators are derived using this embedded boundary framework to increase the order of accuracy of the interior and boundary closure while minimizing the boundary error. Additionally, these novel operators improve the spectral properties on the grid by generalizing to an upwind scheme that has better dispersion relation preserving properties compared to traditional SBP schemes for wave equations. These operators are tested with the curvilinear scalar wave equation on a 3D multiblock grid with an excision sphere embedded in the center block to demonstrate the robustness and accuracy of these novel embedded operators.

</details>


### [154] [Scalarization of Charged Black Hole in Gauss-Bonnet Extended Starobinsky-Maxwell Gravity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14620)
*Rui-Xi Zhu,Hai-Shan Liu*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 在Starobinsky引力耦合Gauss-Bonnet不变量的框架下，重新审视黑洞的自发标量化现象，发现标量化解分裂为两个光滑分支，连接于共同的最小视界半径，这种多分支结构此前未被报道。引入Maxwell场后，该结构仍然存在，且在特定参数范围内出现新的额外不连通分支。所有带毛荷电解均严格满足热力学第一定律。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 揭示Starobinsky引力中黑洞自发标量化的新特征，特别是此前未被发现的多分支结构。

Method: 在Starobinsky引力中引入Gauss-Bonnet不变量，扩展至含Maxwell场情形，通过参数扫描分析标量化解的分支结构及连通性转变。

Result: 发现标量化解存在两个光滑分支，连接于共同的最小视界半径；引入Maxwell场后多分支结构仍存，且在特定参数范围内出现新的不连通分支；所有带毛荷电解均严格满足热力学第一定律。

Conclusion: Starobinsky引力中的黑洞自发标量化展现出丰富的多分支结构，且与热力学第一定律相容。

Abstract: We re-examine spontaneous scalarization of black holes within Starobinsky gravity supplemented by the Gauss-Bonnet invariant. A novel feature is uncovered: scalarized solutions split into two smooth branches that connect at a common minimal horizon radius, a multi-branch structure previously unreported for static, spherically symmetric scalarization. Extending the theory with a Maxwell field, we find that this multi-branch structure persists, while a new additional disconnected branch emerges within certain parameter ranges. We thoroughly analyse the transition from a single smooth branch to two disconnected branches. Lastly, we verify, employing the standard Maxwell thermodynamic relations, that all charged hairy solutions obey the first law exactly.

</details>


### [155] [Random walks around black holes and low-frequency X-ray variability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14626)
*Arthur G. Suvorov*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了强引力场中湍流流体包裹的颗粒在黎曼流形上的随机游走（曲率加权游走），发现黑洞背景下颗粒逃逸概率高于平直空间，并解释了由此产生的X射线变异性谱特征与观测（如天鹅座X-1硬态）相符。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为吸积过程的固有变异性建立理论框架，将嵌入强引力场湍流流体中的颗粒动力学建模为弯曲空间上的随机游走，以解释致密天体吸积的随机性。

Method: 1. 将颗粒随机动力学形式化为黎曼流形上的曲率加权随机游走；2. 求解黑洞背景下的福克-普朗克方程得到逃逸概率；3. 模拟大量初始分布在薄盘结构方程设定的同心壳层上的颗粒轨迹，研究通过视界的粒子通量。

Result: 1. 黑洞背景下颗粒逃逸概率高于平直空间，原因是事件视界附近径向细胞的拉伸提供了更大的空间体积；2. 相较于平直空间，低频段谱指数更浅（时间膨胀主导），高频段更陡；3. 史瓦西加权谱与天鹅座X-1硬态的低频X射线变异性观测广泛匹配。

Conclusion: 几何漂移效应可能对描述随机吸积过程具有重要作用，曲率加权随机游走框架能有效解释黑洞吸积流的观测变异性特征。

Abstract: The stochastic dynamics of a grain embedded within a turbulent fluid subject to strong gravitational fields can be formulated as a random walk on a Riemannian manifold. Such curvature-weighted walks provide a framework to model the intrinsic variability of accretion onto compact objects. By solving the relevant Fokker-Planck equation on a black hole background, we find the counterintuitive result that the escape probability of a grain is actually higher compared to flat space. This is a consequence of the stretching of radial cells near the event horizon: there is a greater spatial volume for the particle to wander through before being captured. By simulating a large number of grain trajectories, initially distributed on concentric shells with a density profile set by the thin-disc structure equations, we also study particle fluxes through the horizon. Shallower spectral indices emerge at low frequencies relative to flat space, primarily due to time dilation, and steeper ones at high frequencies. We find that Schwarzschild-weighted spectra broadly match observations of low-frequency X-ray variability from systems like Cygnus X-1 in their hard state, suggesting that geometric drifts may be important in describing stochastic accretion processes.

</details>


### [156] [Warped Spacelike Singularities and the $C^0$-Inextendibility of Birmingham-Kottler Spacetimes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14741)
*Bobby EkaGunara*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文针对一类翘曲类空奇点，建立了连续洛伦兹延拓的局部障碍判据，并证明了一类Birmingham-Kottler时空的$C^0$不可延拓性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究翘曲类空奇点处的洛伦兹延拓障碍问题，特别是在缺乏球对称性和闭纤维对称性的一般条件下，为时空奇点的不可延拓性提供几何判据。

Method: 通过径向压缩终端因果迹（代替球对称下的旋转形变），在适应边界图中构造紧致时序分隔子；利用纵向平移产生径向切片距离的内在发散与延拓图中的一致控制；结合规范Birmingham-Kottler时空的整体Kruskal坐标及类时测地线的有限固有时间端点分类。

Result: 得到了两个可积性条件、相对翘曲因子的单调性条件及纵向因子发散性构成的局部障碍判据；对非正宇宙学常数的单视界Birmingham-Kottler族，证明了$C^0$不可延拓性，适用于满足特定Ricci曲率条件的任意闭连通纤维（无需齐性、可定向或单连通假设）。

Conclusion: 该局部障碍判据不依赖于闭纤维的对称性；单视界Birmingham-Kottler族在非正宇宙学常数下不可$C^0$延拓。

Abstract: We establish a local obstruction to continuous Lorentzian extensions at a class of warped spacelike singularities. The criterion is expressed through two integrability conditions, a monotonicity condition on the relative warp factors, and divergence of the longitudinal factor; it does not require any symmetry of the closed fiber. The main geometric step is a radial compression of terminal causal traces, which replaces the rotational deformation available in spherical symmetry. The compression yields a compact chronological separator in an adapted boundary chart. Longitudinal translations then produce radial-slice distances that diverge intrinsically while remaining uniformly controlled in the extension chart. Next, we construct the canonical one-horizon Birmingham-Kottler spacetime in global Kruskal coordinates and classify all finite proper-time ends of its timelike geodesics. Every boundary-approaching finite maximizer supplied by a putative extension is thereby forced to the singular end. The local obstruction and the geodesic classification imply $C^0$-inextendibility for the one-horizon Birmingham-Kottler family with nonpositive cosmological constant and every closed connected fiber satisfying $\text{Ric}_{γ_Σ}=(n-2)kγ_Σ$, without assumptions of homogeneity, orientability, or simple connectivity.

</details>


### [157] [Orbital Dynamics and Gravitational-Wave Signatures of EMRIs in Self-Dual Loop Quantum Gravity Black Holes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14746)
*Yu Wang,Meilin Liu,Haiguang Xu*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了极端质量比旋进（EMRIs）在自对偶圈量子引力黑洞时空中的轨道动力学和引力波特征，通过Fisher矩阵分析表明未来空间引力波探测器有望对量子参数施加约束，为探测量子引力效应提供了新途径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 圈量子引力（LQG）预测了对经典黑洞时空的量子修正，这些修正可能在强场区域影响致密天体的动力学和引力波信号。目前缺乏对极端质量比旋进（EMRIs）在LQG修正黑洞背景下的详细研究，本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 在自对偶LQG黑洞几何中分析测试粒子运动，系统性研究有效势和轨道结构；量化量子修正对圆轨道稳定性和强场轨道行为的影响；基于轨道演化构建引力波波形；利用Newman-Janis算法构建旋转扩展的LQG修正Kerr几何；采用Fisher矩阵分析评估未来空间引力波探测器对量子参数的约束能力。

Result: LQG修正改变了近水平轨道动力学；量子效应在长旋进过程中累积，导致与经典情况明显的信号偏差；EMRI观测为探测黑洞时空中的量子引力效应提供了有前景的途径。

Conclusion: 圈量子引力效应在EMRI的长时标演化中可累积并产生可观测的信号偏差，未来空间引力波探测器有望对LQG的两个量子参数（聚合参数P和最小面积参数a₀）施加有意义的约束。

Abstract: Loop quantum gravity (LQG) predicts quantum modifications to classical black-hole spacetimes, which may leave imprints on the dynamics and gravitational-wave signals of compact objects in the strong-field regime. In this work, we investigate the orbital dynamics and gravitational-wave signatures of extreme mass-ratio inspirals (EMRIs) in a self-dual loop quantum gravity black hole spacetime. We analyze test-particle motion in the static, spherically symmetric self-dual LQG geometry characterized by two quantum parameters: the polymeric parameter $P$ and the minimal area parameter $a_0$. The effective potential and orbital structure are systematically studied, and we quantify the influence of quantum corrections on circular-orbit stability and strong-field orbital behavior. Compared with the classical Schwarzschild spacetime, LQG corrections modify the near-horizon orbital dynamics. Based on the orbital evolution, we construct gravitational-wave waveforms and investigate the impact of quantum corrections on waveform morphology. We find that LQG effects accumulate during the long inspiral phase, leading to noticeable signal deviations from the classical case. To incorporate rotation, we construct a rotating extension of the self-dual spacetime using the Newman--Janis algorithm. The resulting LQG-corrected Kerr geometry is used to analyze orbital motion, revealing the interplay between spin and quantum corrections in strong-field trajectories. Finally, we perform a Fisher matrix analysis to estimate potential constraints on quantum parameters from future space-based gravitational-wave detectors. Our results indicate that EMRI observations provide a promising avenue to probe quantum gravitational effects in black-hole spacetimes.

</details>


### [158] [The radial action for massive particles in spherically symmetric geometries: Exact resummation at any PM order](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14802)
*Donato Bini,Giorgio Di Russo*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文计算了多种球对称时空（Schwarzschild、Schwarzschild-Tangherlini及D3-branes）中沿类双曲测地线的大质量粒子径向作用量，展示了其在eikonal极限下用特殊函数（超几何、Fox-Wright函数）进行重求和的性质，并推广了先前零测地线结果。在4维Schwarzschild黑洞中，利用量子Seiberg-Witten曲线方法，证明了量子a-圈与径向作用量的简单关系，并给出了完全重求和表达式，同时给出了对偶a_D-圈的展开形式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将先前针对零测地线（无质量粒子）的径向作用量重求和结果推广到大质量粒子情形，并利用量子Seiberg-Witten曲线方法探索径向作用量与量子a-圈之间的关系。

Method: 1) 在eikonal极限下，利用超几何函数和Fox-Wright函数对径向作用量进行重求和；2) 在4维Schwarzschild时空中，采用量子Seiberg-Witten曲线方法处理大质量标量场的径向方程，建立量子a-圈（重正化角动量）与径向作用量的联系。

Result: 得到了大质量粒子径向作用量在多种球对称时空中的重求和表达式；证明了4维Schwarzschild时空中量子a-圈与径向作用量在质量情形下的简单关系，并给出了完全重求和形式；给出了对偶a_D-圈的显式展开表达式，但尚未得到其重求和形式。

Conclusion: 成功将径向作用量的重求和性质从零测地线推广到大质量粒子情形，并建立了与量子Seiberg-Witten曲线方法的联系，但a_D-圈的重求和仍有待解决。

Abstract: We compute the massive particles radial action along hyperboliclike geodesics in various spherically symmetric spacetimes: the standard $4d$ Schwarzschild spacetime, its $d$-dimensional genralization known as Schwarzschild-Tangherlini solutions and for the D3-branes spacetimes, showing useful resummation properties in terms of special (hypergeometric, Fox-Wright) functions in the eikonal limit and generalizing previous results valid for null geodesics. As a consequence, the scattering angle can be resummed too, and we explicitly display the resummed expressions.
  In addition, in the more interesting situation of a $4d$ Schwarzschild black hole spacetime, following the approach of the quantum Seiberg-Witten curves to the radial equation associated with a massive scalar field, we show that the quantum $a$-cycle (or, equivalently, the \lq\lq renormalized angular momentum") is simply related to radial action also in this massive case, providing fully resummed expressions. Finally, we display the explicit, expanded-form expression of the dual $a_D$-cycle, for which, however, no resummed expressions have been derived yet.

</details>


### [159] [Chaos bound violation by spinning particles in Gauss-Bonnet-AdS black holes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14863)
*Xiaowei Li,Mengxuan Wu,Guoping Li*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了自旋测试粒子在高斯-博内反德西特时空中混沌边界的违反现象，发现粒子自旋、高斯-博内参数和时空维度对Lyapunov指数有显著影响，并揭示了这些因素在修正引力理论中控制混沌边界有效性的重要作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究修正引力理论中混沌边界的有效性，特别是高斯-博内引力和高维时空对自旋粒子混沌行为的影响机制。

Method: 通过分析五维、八维和九维高斯-博内反德西特时空中自旋测试粒子的Lyapunov指数，研究自旋、高斯-博内参数、时空维度和角动量对混沌边界违反的调节作用。

Result: 五维时空中Lyapunov指数随粒子自旋单调增长；八维和九维时空呈现非单调行为（先减后增）；高斯-博内参数显著调节近视界几何从而影响边界违反；角动量增加增强混沌；但在小参数条件下，维度主导效应。

Conclusion: 时空维度和高斯-博内参数是修正引力理论中控制混沌边界的两个重要因素。

Abstract: In this work, we investigate the violation of the chaos bound for spinning test particles in Gauss-Bonnet anti-de Sitter spacetime, focusing on the regulatory roles of the Gauss-Bonnet parameter and spacetime dimensionality. In five-dimensional space time, the Lyapunov exponent grows monotonically with particle spin. In contrast, in eight- and nine-dimensional spacetimes, it exhibits non-monotonic behavior-first decreasing and then increasing-reflecting the nonlinear nature of higher-dimensional tensor couplings. The Gauss-Bonnet parameter significantly modulates the violation by reshaping the near-horizon geometry: in five dimensions, the deviation of the Lyapunov exponent from the surface gravity first increases and then decreases with the Gauss-Bonnet parameter, whereas in the higher dimensions the bound is more easily violated. Increasing the total angular momentum of the particle also enhances chaos; however, when the Gauss-Bonnet parameter and black hole charge are small, the spacetime dimensionality, rather than the angular momentum, dominates. These results establish the spacetime dimensionality and the Gauss-Bonnet parameter as important factors governing the validity of the chaos bound in modified gravity theories.

</details>


### [160] [Kerr black holes without primary hairs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14910)
*J Ovalle*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文提出了一类由参数{ℳ, a}完全表征的正则轴对称黑洞几何族，该族包含Kerr事件视界，在正则时空、可积奇点时空和Kerr解之间内插，具有准极端配置和描述Kerr黑洞形成过程的特点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 寻找一类能够连接正则时空与Kerr黑洞解，且具有Kerr事件视界的轴对称黑洞几何，以探索准极端配置和黑洞形成过程的解析描述。

Method: 通过参数{ℳ, a}构造一类正则轴对称黑洞几何族，要求其包含Kerr事件视界，并在正则时空、可积奇点时空与Kerr解之间进行插值。

Result: 该族几何包含准极端配置（无需a≈ℳ），并提供了从初始正则配置到Kerr黑洞形成的解析框架。

Conclusion: 该工作为理解正则黑洞与Kerr解之间的关系提供了新视角，并为解析描述Kerr黑洞形成过程奠定了基础。

Abstract: We present a class of regular axisymmetric black hole geometries fully characterized by the parameters $\{{\cal M},a\}$ and possessing the Kerr event horizon. This family interpolates between regular spacetimes, configurations with integrable singularities, and the Kerr solution as a limiting case. Its main features are: (i) the existence of quasi-extremal configurations without requiring $a \approx {\cal M}$; and (ii) a possible framework toward an analytical description of Kerr black hole formation from an initially regular configuration.

</details>


### [161] [Comment on "Non-linear interactions in cosmologies with energy exchange"](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15012)
*Naman Soni*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文对Eur. Phys. J. C 80, 120 (2020)一文中关于宇宙学模型的数学推导进行了批判性重新评估，指出并纠正了包括Liénard型方程的简化错误、积分常数的不当省略以及参数变易法的错误使用等若干数学不一致性。通过推导精确解析解，证明修正后的数学框架与原文章的核心主张存在根本矛盾。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 指出并纠正原始文章在具有能量交换的宇宙学模型中存在的数学不一致性，包括Liénard型方程的错误简化、积分常数的遗漏以及参数变易法的误用。

Method: 通过重新审视原始文章的数学推导，识别出具体的数学错误（错误的简化、遗漏积分常数、参数变易法误用），并推导演绎出精确的解析解以验证修正后的数学框架。

Result: 发现了原始文章中的多处数学错误，并给出了修正后的精确解析解；修正后的数学框架与原文章的核心主张存在根本性矛盾。

Conclusion: 原始文章基于有缺陷的数学推导得出的结论是不可靠的，修正后的结果从根本上否定了原文章的核心主张。

Abstract: This work provides a critical reassessment of the cosmological models presented in the article "Non-linear interactions in cosmologies with energy exchange" Eur. Phys. J. C 80, 120 (2020) arXiv:1907.06410 . It points out and corrects several mathematical inconsistencies in the original article. These include a flawed simplification of a Liénard-type equation, unjustified omissions of integration constants, and an incorrect use of the variation-of parameters method. By deriving the exact analytical solutions, it is shown that the corrected mathematical framework fundamentally contradicts the claims of the original article.

</details>


### [162] [Noncommutative black holes: Topological bulk-boundary correspondence and Binary Merger Bounds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15150)
*Ankit Anand,Anshul Mishra,Aditya Singh,Saeed Noori Gashti,Neeraj Kumar,Phongpichit Channuie*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了洛伦兹涂抹非对易时空中带电AdS黑洞的热力学拓扑，通过微扰展开和数值分析揭示了非对易效应对相变拓扑结构及残余质量的修正，并证明了体-边界拓扑对应关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 由于非对易时空中精确解析解不可得，传统热力学分析方法受限，需要发展微扰方法研究非对易效应对黑洞热力学相变拓扑结构的定性影响。

Method: 采用非对易参数的微扰展开推导临界热力学量，结合数值分析验证；利用广义离壳自由能框架分析热力学相空间的拓扑结构，计算绕数表征相变。

Result: 非对易效应在定性上改变了标准Reissner-Nordström AdS黑洞的热力学行为；体描述与边界描述的全局热力学拓扑完全一致，验证了拓扑对应；非对易修正了熵和最终黑洞质量等关键热力学量，并对第二定律给出的残余质量下限产生影响。

Conclusion: 非对易时空修正了带电AdS黑洞的热力学拓扑结构，体-边界拓扑对应成立，残余质量下限受非对易效应调制。

Abstract: We investigate the thermodynamic topology of charged AdS black holes in a non-commutative spacetime sourced by Lorentzian-smeared matter distributions. Since exact analytical solutions for the critical thermodynamic quantities are not available, we employ a perturbative expansion in the non-commutative parameter and validate the resulting expressions through numerical analysis. Using the generalized off-shell free-energy framework, we explore the topological structure of the thermodynamic phase space and evaluate the corresponding winding number that characterizes the phase transitions. Our results reveal that non-commutative effects introduce qualitative modifications to the thermodynamic behavior compared with the standard Reissner-Nordström AdS black hole. Furthermore, we demonstrate that the bulk and boundary descriptions possess an identical global thermodynamic topology, providing strong evidence for the correspondence between their topological structures. We also investigate the lower bound on the remnant mass implied by the second law of black-hole thermodynamics and observe that non-commutative corrections modify key thermodynamic quantities, with particular emphasis on the entropy and the final black-hole mass.

</details>


### [163] [Nonlinear Gravitational Wave Interactions in an Expanding Universe](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15170)
*Fan Zhang,Lee Lindblom,Zhi-Peng Peng*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本研究通过数值模拟探索了膨胀宇宙中引力波的非线性相互作用，发现引力波谱的能量会从初始峰值波长向更长和更短波长级联，且级联行为与四波散射模型预测一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解膨胀宇宙中引力波的非线性相互作用及其能量级联机制，验证四波散射模型在引力波湍流中的适用性。

Method: 在空间体积增长超过一个数量级的宇宙学模型中对随机引力波系综进行数值演化，并在不同引力波振幅下进行演化实验。

Result: 引力波谱的能量从初始峰值波长向更长和更短波长级联，不同振幅下的级联行为与四波散射模型预测的振幅缩放关系一致。

Conclusion: 引力波湍流中存在非线性相互作用导致的能量级联过程，四波散射模型能够有效描述该过程的行为。

Abstract: This study explores the nonlinear interactions between gravitational waves in an expanding universe. Random ensembles of gravitational waves are evolved numerically in cosmological models whose spatial volumes increase by more than an order of magnitude during these simulations. These evolutions show the spectra of the gravitational waves evolving as energy cascades away from the wavelengths at the peak of the initial spectra toward both longer and shorter wavelengths. Evolutions performed at different gravitational wave amplitudes are shown to produce cascades that scale with amplitude in the way predicted by the four-wave scattering models of gravitational-wave turbulence.

</details>


<div id='physics.comp-ph'></div>

# physics.comp-ph [[Back]](#toc)

### [164] [A fast summation method for the DFT-D3 dispersion correction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15103)
*Victoria Valeeva,Cheuk Hin Ho,Mario Geiger,Franco Pellegrini,Gábor Csányi,Emine Kucukbenli,Christoph Ortner*

Main category: physics.comp-ph

TL;DR: FourierD3通过功能低秩分解恢复DFT-D3色散修正的可分离性，实现O(N log N)的粒子网格评估，无需实空间截断。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DFT-D3色散校正的环境依赖成对系数破坏了原子中心可分离性，导致快速求和方法无法直接应用，迫使研究者要么截断D3要么接受显著的速度降低。

Method: 采用功能低秩分解（functional low-rank decomposition）来恢复原子中心可分离性，使得色散和能够通过粒子网格方法（particle-mesh evaluation）在O(N log N)时间内完成计算，无需实空间截断。

Result: 实现了O(N log N)时间复杂度的色散求和计算，消除了对实空间截断的依赖。

Conclusion: FourierD3方法有效解决了DFT-D3在机器学习力场中的计算效率瓶颈，通过可分离性恢复实现了快速、无截断的色散校正计算。

Abstract: The DFT-D3 dispersion correction is routinely added to machine learning force fields (MLFFs) trained on dispersion-deficient functionals such as PBE. Its environment-dependent pair coefficients, however, break the atom-centered separability that fast summation methods require, forcing practitioners either to truncate D3 or to accept a substantial slowdown. We introduce FourierD3, a method that uses a functional low-rank decomposition to restore this separability and enable particle-mesh evaluation in $O(N\log N)$ time without a real-space cutoff on the dispersion sum.

</details>


### [165] [Translation of transient acoustic fields](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15138)
*Michael J. Carley*

Main category: physics.comp-ph

TL;DR: 提出了一种利用球谐展开和Kirchhoff-Helmholtz积分实现声场数据从源区域到目标区域平移的"point and shoot"方法，主要应用于时域快速多极子方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 主要动机是将该方法应用于时域快速多极子方法（FMM），实现声场数据在源区域和目标区域之间的高效平移。

Method: 将声场数据表示为源区域和目标区域周围球面上的球谐展开，利用"point and shoot"方法结合Kirchhoff-Helmholtz积分实现从一个球面到另一个球面的轴向平移。

Result: 在合适的参数下方法收敛到六位精度，计算复杂度约为O(N²)，其中N为球谐展开的阶数。

Conclusion: 该方法在时域快速多极子方法的应用场景下有效，能够以可控的计算代价实现高精度的声场平移。

Abstract: A method is presented for the translation of acoustic field data from a source to a target region. Field data are represented as spherical harmonic expansions on spheres surrounding the source and target regions respectively and expansions are translated using a ``point and shoot'' method using the Kirchhoff-Helmholtz integral to carry out an axial translation from one sphere to the other. The principal motivation for the method is its use in a time-domain Fast Multipole Method, and test cases reflective of this application are presented. The method converges to six digits for appropriate values of parameters and computational effort scales approximately as $N^{2}$ where $N$ is the order of spherical harmonic expansion for the field data.

</details>
